- Produces:
  - `internal sealed class VertexGrid` and `internal readonly record struct GridPoint(long X, long Y)` in `Lod.Core.Geometry`: `Key(Point2)`, `Register(Point2)`, `Original(GridPoint)`, `Keys`, and the static tests `Parameter`, `IsStrictlyInside`, `IsOnSegment`, moved unchanged from the private `Grid` of `LayoutSurfaceBuilder`.
  - `public IReadOnlyList<Polygon2> PolygonOps.UnionTiles(IReadOnlyList<Polygon2> tiles)` (D-076): vertices in one cell of the distance grid are unified and every vertex lying on another tile's edge (a T-junction) is inserted into that edge before `Union`; a tile whose rings collapse on the grid is clipped as given.
  - `LayoutSurfaceBuilder` uses `VertexGrid`; its output is unchanged. `SingleZoneMerge` builds each storey outline with `UnionTiles`.

```csharp
using System;
using System.Linq;
using Lod.Core.Common;
using Lod.Core.Geometry;
using Xunit;

namespace Lod.Core.Tests.Geometry;

public sealed class GeometryTests
{
    private readonly PolygonOps _ops = new(ToleranceSettings.Default);

    [Fact]
    public void PolygonNormalisesOrientationAndComputesAreaWithHoles()
    {
        Polygon2 polygon = Polygon2.Create(
            new[] { new Point2(0, 0), new Point2(0, 10), new Point2(10, 10), new Point2(10, 0) },
            new[] { new[] { new Point2(2, 2), new Point2(4, 2), new Point2(4, 4), new Point2(2, 4) } }).Value;

        Assert.True(Polygon2.SignedArea(polygon.Outer) > 0);
        Assert.True(Polygon2.SignedArea(polygon.Holes[0]) < 0);
        Assert.Equal(96.0, polygon.Area, 12);
    }

    [Fact]
    public void PolygonDropsClosingAndRepeatedPoints()
    {
        Polygon2 polygon = Polygon2.Create(new[] { new Point2(0, 0), new Point2(1, 0), new Point2(1, 0), new Point2(1, 1), new Point2(0, 0) }).Value;

        Assert.Equal(3, polygon.Outer.Count);
    }

    [Fact]
    public void PolygonRejectsDegenerateRings()
    {
        Assert.Contains(
            Polygon2.Create(new[] { new Point2(0, 0), new Point2(1, 0), new Point2(2, 0) }).Diagnostics,
            d => d.Code == DiagnosticCodes.DegeneratePolygon);
    }

    [Fact]
    public void IntersectionOfOverlappingRectangles()
    {
        double area = _ops.IntersectionArea(Polygon2.Rectangle(0, 0, 10, 10), Polygon2.Rectangle(5, 5, 15, 15));

        Assert.Equal(25.0, area, 9);
    }

    [Fact]
    public void TouchingRectanglesDoNotIntersect()
    {
        Assert.Empty(_ops.Intersect(Polygon2.Rectangle(0, 0, 10, 10), Polygon2.Rectangle(10, 0, 20, 10)));
    }

    [Fact]
    public void UnionOfAdjacentRectanglesIsOnePolygon()
    {
        Polygon2 union = Assert.Single(_ops.Union(new[] { Polygon2.Rectangle(0, 0, 10, 5), Polygon2.Rectangle(10, 0, 20, 5) }));

        Assert.Equal(100.0, union.Area, 9);
    }

    [Fact]
    public void DifferenceKeepsHoles()
    {
        Polygon2 ring = Assert.Single(_ops.Difference(Polygon2.Rectangle(0, 0, 10, 10), new[] { Polygon2.Rectangle(3, 3, 7, 7) }));

        Assert.Single(ring.Holes);
        Assert.Equal(84.0, ring.Area, 9);
    }

    [Theory]
    [InlineData(0, 0, 10, 0, 180.0)]
    [InlineData(10, 0, 10, 10, 90.0)]
    [InlineData(10, 10, 0, 10, 0.0)]
    [InlineData(0, 10, 0, 0, 270.0)]
    public void OutwardAzimuthOfCounterClockwiseRectangleEdges(double x1, double y1, double x2, double y2, double azimuth)
    {
        Assert.Equal(azimuth, Orientation.OutwardAzimuth(new Point2(x1, y1), new Point2(x2, y2)), 9);
    }

    [Theory]
    [InlineData(0.0, OrientationBin.North)]
    [InlineData(44.9, OrientationBin.North)]
    [InlineData(45.0, OrientationBin.East)]
    [InlineData(180.0, OrientationBin.South)]
    [InlineData(270.0, OrientationBin.West)]
    [InlineData(315.0, OrientationBin.North)]
    [InlineData(-90.0, OrientationBin.West)]
    public void OrientationBins(double azimuth, OrientationBin bin)
    {
        Assert.Equal(bin, Orientation.Bin(azimuth));
    }

    [Fact]
    public void CenteredWindowScalesTheWallRectangle()
    {
        Window window = Window.Centered(10.0, 3.0, 0.25 * 30.0)!;

        Assert.Equal(5.0, window.Width, 12);
        Assert.Equal(1.5, window.Height, 12);
        Assert.Equal(0.75, window.SillHeight, 12);
        Assert.Equal(7.5, window.Area, 12);
    }

    [Fact]
    public void ZeroGlazingHasNoWindow()
    {
        Assert.Null(Window.Centered(10.0, 3.0, 0.0));
    }

    [Fact]
    public void GlazingMustBeSmallerThanTheWall()
    {
        Assert.Throws<ArgumentOutOfRangeException>(() => Window.Centered(10.0, 3.0, 30.0));
    }

    [Fact]
    public void TranslateMovesEveryRing()
    {
        Polygon2 moved = Polygon2.Rectangle(0, 0, 1, 1).Translate(2, 3);

        Assert.Equal(2.0, moved.Outer.Min(p => p.X));
        Assert.Equal(3.0, moved.Outer.Min(p => p.Y));
    }

    [Fact]
    public void UnionOfRotatedTilesWithTJunctionsIsOneOutline()
    {
        // A 4 m x 18 m strip and two 20 m x 9 m rectangles beside it: the rectangles' corners at (4, 9) lie on the strip's edge (a T-junction).
        // Rotated by 7.3°, rounding on the clipping grid moves that corner off the edge, which a plain union turns into a slit or a split.
        var motion = new PlanMotion(7.3, 0.0, 0.0);
        Polygon2[] tiles =
        {
            Polygon2.Rectangle(0, 0, 4, 18).Transform(motion),
            Polygon2.Rectangle(4, 0, 24, 9).Transform(motion),
            Polygon2.Rectangle(4, 9, 24, 18).Transform(motion),
        };

        Polygon2 outline = Assert.Single(_ops.UnionTiles(tiles));

        Assert.Empty(outline.Holes);
        Assert.Equal(432.0, outline.Area, 5);
        var back = new PlanMotion(-7.3, 0.0, 0.0);
        Assert.All(outline.Outer.Select(back.Apply), p => Assert.True(
            Math.Abs(p.X) < 1e-5 || Math.Abs(p.X - 24) < 1e-5 || Math.Abs(p.Y) < 1e-5 || Math.Abs(p.Y - 18) < 1e-5,
            $"({p.X}, {p.Y}) is not on the outline"));
    }
}
```

Replace `tests/Lod.Integration.Tests/PlanTransformTests.cs` with the following full file. The theory `EveryCombinationValidatesOnARotatedAndTranslatedPlan` is added: every plan simplifier with every floor aggregator, Multipliers 1, 3, 1, on the canonical plan rotated and moved by four placements (30° and (5.3, −2.1), 7.3° in place, 131.4° and (15.08, −153.9), 248.2° and (42.62, −245.2)), 64 cases, each simplifying and aggregating without error and without warning and validating.

```csharp
using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.Linq;
using Lod.Core.Buildings;
using Lod.Core.Common;
using Lod.Core.Geometry;
using Lod.Core.Model;
using Lod.Core.Plans;
using Lod.Core.Simplification;
using Lod.Core.Validation;
using Xunit;

namespace Lod.Integration.Tests;

public sealed class PlanTransformTests
{
    [Theory]
    [InlineData(0.0)]
    [InlineData(90.0)]
    [InlineData(30.0)]
    public void RotationKeepsAreaVolumeWallAreaAndGlazing(double degrees)
    {
        IGeneratedPlan plan = Pipeline.Plan();

        IGeneratedPlan moved = PlanTransform.Apply(plan, degrees, 5.3, -2.1).Value;
        Totals before = Totals.Of(plan.Zones, plan.Surfaces, plan.OrientationDegrees);
        Totals after = Totals.Of(moved.Zones, moved.Surfaces, moved.OrientationDegrees);

        Assert.True(Pipeline.Tolerances.AreaEquals(plan.Footprint.Area, moved.Footprint.Area));
        Assert.True(Pipeline.Tolerances.AreaEquals(before.FloorArea, after.FloorArea));
        Assert.True(Pipeline.Tolerances.AreaEquals(before.Volume, after.Volume));
        Assert.True(Pipeline.Tolerances.AreaEquals(before.ExteriorWallArea, after.ExteriorWallArea));
        Assert.True(Pipeline.Tolerances.AreaEquals(before.Glazing, after.Glazing));
        Assert.Equal(plan.Zones.Select(z => z.Id), moved.Zones.Select(z => z.Id));
        Assert.Equal(plan.Surfaces.Select(s => s.Id), moved.Surfaces.Select(s => s.Id));
        Assert.Equal(plan.OrientationDegrees, moved.OrientationDegrees);
        Assert.Equal(plan.FloorHeight, moved.FloorHeight);
    }

    [Fact]
    public void GlazingPerOrientationFollowsTheRotatedFacades()
    {
        IGeneratedPlan plan = Pipeline.Plan();
        IReadOnlyDictionary<OrientationBin, double> before = Totals.Of(plan.Zones, plan.Surfaces, plan.OrientationDegrees).GlazingByOrientation;

        IGeneratedPlan quarter = PlanTransform.Apply(plan, 90.0, 0.0, 0.0).Value;
        IGeneratedPlan thirty = PlanTransform.Apply(plan, 30.0, 0.0, 0.0).Value;
        IReadOnlyDictionary<OrientationBin, double> afterQuarter = Totals.Of(quarter.Zones, quarter.Surfaces, quarter.OrientationDegrees).GlazingByOrientation;
        IReadOnlyDictionary<OrientationBin, double> afterThirty = Totals.Of(thirty.Zones, thirty.Surfaces, thirty.OrientationDegrees).GlazingByOrientation;

        // A quarter turn counter-clockwise turns a north façade west, east north, south east, and west south.
        Assert.Equal(before[OrientationBin.North], afterQuarter[OrientationBin.West], 9);
        Assert.Equal(before[OrientationBin.East], afterQuarter[OrientationBin.North], 9);
        Assert.Equal(before[OrientationBin.South], afterQuarter[OrientationBin.East], 9);
        Assert.Equal(before[OrientationBin.West], afterQuarter[OrientationBin.South], 9);

        // 30° keeps every façade azimuth inside its 90° bin (0° → 330°, 90° → 60°, ...).
        Assert.All(before.Keys, bin => Assert.Equal(before[bin], afterThirty[bin], 9));
    }

    [Fact]
    public void WallFacingIsRecomputedFromTheRotatedGeometry()
    {
        IGeneratedPlan plan = Pipeline.Plan();

        IGeneratedPlan moved = PlanTransform.Apply(plan, 90.0, 0.0, 0.0).Value;

        WallSurface[] original = plan.Surfaces.OfType<WallSurface>().ToArray();
        WallSurface[] rotated = moved.Surfaces.OfType<WallSurface>().ToArray();
        Assert.Equal(original.Length, rotated.Length);
        for (int i = 0; i < original.Length; i++)
        {
            Assert.Equal(Orientation.Normalise(original[i].PlanAzimuth - 90.0), rotated[i].PlanAzimuth, 9);
            Assert.Equal(original[i].Windows.Select(w => (w.Offset, w.SillHeight, w.Width, w.Height)), rotated[i].Windows.Select(w => (w.Offset, w.SillHeight, w.Width, w.Height)));
        }

        WallSurface northFacing = original.First(w => w.Boundary == BoundaryCondition.Outdoors && Orientation.Bin(w.PlanAzimuth) == OrientationBin.North);
        Assert.Equal(OrientationBin.West, Orientation.Bin(rotated[Array.IndexOf(original, northFacing)].PlanAzimuth));
    }

    [Fact]
    public void TranslationOnlyMovesEveryPoint()
    {
        IGeneratedPlan plan = Pipeline.Plan();

        IGeneratedPlan moved = PlanTransform.Apply(plan, 0.0, 5.3, -2.1).Value;

        Assert.Equal(plan.Footprint.Outer.Select(p => new Point2(p.X + 5.3, p.Y - 2.1)), moved.Footprint.Outer);
        Assert.Equal(
            plan.Zones.SelectMany(z => z.Parts).SelectMany(p => p.Footprint.Outer).Select(p => new Point2(p.X + 5.3, p.Y - 2.1)),
            moved.Zones.SelectMany(z => z.Parts).SelectMany(p => p.Footprint.Outer));
        Assert.Equal(
            plan.Surfaces.OfType<WallSurface>().Select(w => (new Point2(w.Start.X + 5.3, w.Start.Y - 2.1), new Point2(w.End.X + 5.3, w.End.Y - 2.1), w.Elevation, w.Height)),
            moved.Surfaces.OfType<WallSurface>().Select(w => (w.Start, w.End, w.Elevation, w.Height)));
        Assert.Equal(
            plan.Surfaces.OfType<HorizontalSurface>().SelectMany(h => h.Polygon.Outer).Select(p => new Point2(p.X + 5.3, p.Y - 2.1)),
            moved.Surfaces.OfType<HorizontalSurface>().SelectMany(h => h.Polygon.Outer));
        Assert.All(moved.Surfaces.OfType<HorizontalSurface>(), h => Assert.Equal(BoundaryCondition.Unresolved, h.Boundary));
    }

    [Fact]
    public void TransformIsDeterministicAndRecordedInProvenance()
    {
        IGeneratedPlan plan = Pipeline.Plan();

        IGeneratedPlan first = PlanTransform.Apply(plan, 30.0, 5.3, -2.1).Value;
        IGeneratedPlan second = PlanTransform.Apply(plan, 30.0, 5.3, -2.1).Value;

        Assert.Equal(TextReport.Describe(first), TextReport.Describe(second));
        Assert.Equal("TransformPlan", first.Provenance.Operation);
        Assert.Equal(
            new[] { "RotationDegrees=30", "TranslationX=5.3", "TranslationY=-2.1" },
            first.Provenance.Parameters.Select(p => $"{p.Key}={p.Value}"));
        Assert.Same(plan.Provenance, Assert.Single(first.Provenance.Inputs));
    }

    [Theory]
    [InlineData(double.NaN, 0.0, 0.0)]
    [InlineData(0.0, double.PositiveInfinity, 0.0)]
    [InlineData(0.0, 0.0, double.NegativeInfinity)]
    [InlineData(double.PositiveInfinity, double.NaN, double.NaN)]
    public void NonFiniteInputsAreInvalidParameters(double degrees, double x, double y)
    {
        Result<IGeneratedPlan> result = PlanTransform.Apply(Pipeline.Plan(), degrees, x, y);

        Assert.False(result.IsSuccess);
        Assert.All(result.Diagnostics, d => Assert.Equal(DiagnosticCodes.InvalidParameter, d.Code));
        Assert.Equal(new[] { degrees, x, y }.Count(v => double.IsNaN(v) || double.IsInfinity(v)), result.Diagnostics.Count);
    }

    public static IEnumerable<object[]> Matrix() =>
        from placement in new[] { (30.0, 5.3, -2.1), (7.3, 0.0, 0.0), (131.4, 15.08, -153.9), (248.2, 42.62, -245.2) }
        from simplifier in new[] { "NoSimplification", "SemanticMerge", "PerimeterCore", "SingleZonePerFloor" }
        from aggregator in new[] { "Stack", "StackedFloorZoneMultiplier", "SingleZonePerFloorType", "SingleZoneBuilding" }
        select new object[] { placement.Item1, placement.Item2, placement.Item3, simplifier, aggregator };

    [Theory]
    [MemberData(nameof(Matrix))]
    public void EveryCombinationValidatesOnARotatedAndTranslatedPlan(double degrees, double x, double y, string simplifier, string aggregator)
    {
        IGeneratedPlan plan = PlanTransform.Apply(Pipeline.Plan(), degrees, x, y).Value;
        Result<IFloor> floor = Pipeline.Simplifier(simplifier).Simplify(plan);
        Assert.True(floor.IsSuccess, string.Join("; ", floor.Diagnostics));
        FloorEntry[] entries = { new(floor.Value, 1), new(floor.Value, 3), new(floor.Value, 1) };

        Result<IGeneratedBuilding> building = Pipeline.Aggregator(aggregator).Aggregate(entries);
        Assert.True(building.IsSuccess, string.Join("; ", building.Diagnostics));
        ValidationReport report = new BuildingValidator(Pipeline.Tolerances).Validate(building.Value);

        Assert.True(report.Passed, report.Describe());
        Assert.DoesNotContain(building.Diagnostics, d => d.Severity == DiagnosticSeverity.Warning);
    }

    [Theory]
    [InlineData("Stack")]
    [InlineData("StackedFloorZoneMultiplier")]
    [InlineData("SingleZonePerFloorType")]
    [InlineData("SingleZoneBuilding")]
    public void AStoreyMovedOffTheStoreyBelowIsUnsupported(string aggregator)
    {
        // The upper plan is moved by (5.3, -2.1): it overlaps the lower 24 m x 18 m plan on 18.7 m x 15.9 m = 297.33 m², so 134.67 m² overhang.
        IFloor lower = new NoSimplification().Simplify(Pipeline.Plan()).Value;
        IFloor upper = new NoSimplification().Simplify(PlanTransform.Apply(Pipeline.Plan(), 0.0, 5.3, -2.1).Value).Value;

        Result<IGeneratedBuilding> result = Pipeline.Aggregator(aggregator).Aggregate(new[] { new FloorEntry(lower, 2), new FloorEntry(upper, 2) });

        Diagnostic warning = Assert.Single(result.Diagnostics, d => d.Code == DiagnosticCodes.UnsupportedStorey);
        Assert.Equal(DiagnosticSeverity.Warning, warning.Severity);
        Assert.Equal("L2", warning.Subject);
        Assert.Contains("134.670000 m²", warning.Message);
        ValidationReport report = new BuildingValidator(Pipeline.Tolerances).Validate(result.Value);
        Assert.True(report.Passed, report.Describe());
        Totals totals = Totals.Of(result.Value.Zones, result.Value.Surfaces, result.Value.OrientationDegrees);
        Assert.Equal(134.67, totals.ExposedFloorArea, 6);
        Assert.Equal(432.0 + 134.67, totals.RoofArea, 6);
        Assert.Equal(432.0, totals.GroundArea, 6);
    }
}
```

- [x] **Step 3: Extract the vertex grid — create `src/Lod.Core/Geometry/VertexGrid.cs` (full file)**

```csharp
using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;

namespace Lod.Core.Geometry;

/// <summary>A cell of the <see cref="VertexGrid"/>, in grid units.</summary>
/// <param name="X">X index.</param>
/// <param name="Y">Y index.</param>
internal readonly record struct GridPoint(long X, long Y);

/// <summary>
/// Snaps points to the distance grid, remembers the first original point of each grid cell, and tests whether a grid point lies on a segment
/// within one grid unit. Shared by the layout surface builder and the union of tiles, so both recognise the same vertices and T-junctions.
/// </summary>
internal sealed class VertexGrid
{
    private readonly double _cell;
    private readonly Dictionary<GridPoint, Point2> _originals = new();

    /// <summary>Creates an empty grid.</summary>
    /// <param name="cell">Grid spacing, m: the distance tolerance.</param>
    public VertexGrid(double cell)
    {
        _cell = cell;
    }

    /// <summary>Registered cells, in registration order.</summary>
    public IEnumerable<GridPoint> Keys => _originals.Keys;

    /// <summary>The cell of a point.</summary>
    /// <param name="p">The point.</param>
    /// <returns>The cell.</returns>
    public GridPoint Key(Point2 p) => new((long)Math.Round(p.X / _cell, MidpointRounding.AwayFromZero), (long)Math.Round(p.Y / _cell, MidpointRounding.AwayFromZero));

    /// <summary>Registers a point; the first point registered in a cell stands for the cell.</summary>
    /// <param name="p">The point.</param>
    public void Register(Point2 p)
    {
        GridPoint key = Key(p);
        if (!_originals.ContainsKey(key))
        {
            _originals.Add(key, p);
        }
    }

    /// <summary>The first point registered in a cell.</summary>
    /// <param name="key">The cell.</param>
    /// <returns>The point.</returns>
    public Point2 Original(GridPoint key) => _originals[key];

    /// <summary>Position of <paramref name="p"/> along <paramref name="a"/>–<paramref name="b"/>, 0 at <paramref name="a"/> and 1 at <paramref name="b"/>.</summary>
    /// <param name="a">Segment start.</param>
    /// <param name="b">Segment end.</param>
    /// <param name="p">The point.</param>
    /// <returns>The parameter.</returns>
    public static double Parameter(GridPoint a, GridPoint b, GridPoint p)
    {
        double dx = b.X - a.X;
        double dy = b.Y - a.Y;
        return (((p.X - a.X) * dx) + ((p.Y - a.Y) * dy)) / ((dx * dx) + (dy * dy));
    }

    /// <summary>Within one grid unit of the line and strictly between the end points (by more than half a grid unit).</summary>
    /// <param name="a">Segment start.</param>
    /// <param name="b">Segment end.</param>
    /// <param name="p">The point.</param>
    /// <returns>Whether <paramref name="p"/> splits the segment.</returns>
    public static bool IsStrictlyInside(GridPoint a, GridPoint b, GridPoint p)
    {
        if (DistanceToLine(a, b, p) > 1.0)
        {
            return false;
        }

        double length = Math.Sqrt(Math.Pow(b.X - a.X, 2) + Math.Pow(b.Y - a.Y, 2));
        double along = Parameter(a, b, p) * length;
        return along > 0.5 && along < length - 0.5;
    }

    /// <summary>Within one grid unit of the segment, end points included.</summary>
    /// <param name="a">Segment start.</param>
    /// <param name="b">Segment end.</param>
    /// <param name="p">The point.</param>
    /// <returns>Whether <paramref name="p"/> lies on the segment.</returns>
    public static bool IsOnSegment(GridPoint a, GridPoint b, GridPoint p)
    {
        if (p == a || p == b)
        {
            return true;
        }

        double length = Math.Sqrt(Math.Pow(b.X - a.X, 2) + Math.Pow(b.Y - a.Y, 2));
        double along = Parameter(a, b, p) * length;
        return DistanceToLine(a, b, p) <= 1.0 && along >= -1.0 && along <= length + 1.0;
    }

    private static double DistanceToLine(GridPoint a, GridPoint b, GridPoint p)
    {
        double dx = b.X - a.X;
        double dy = b.Y - a.Y;
        return Math.Abs((dx * (p.Y - a.Y)) - (dy * (p.X - a.X))) / Math.Sqrt((dx * dx) + (dy * dy));
    }
}
```

```csharp
using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.Linq;
using Lod.Core.Common;
using Lod.Core.Geometry;
using Lod.Core.Model;

namespace Lod.Core.Layout;

/// <summary>A zone footprint to build surfaces for.</summary>
/// <param name="Id">Zone ID.</param>
/// <param name="Footprint">Zone floor polygon.</param>
public sealed record LayoutZone(ZoneId Id, Polygon2 Footprint);

/// <summary>
/// Builds the surfaces of a single-storey layout: walls split wherever another zone's vertex touches them, paired across zones
/// (interzone), or on the plan boundary (outdoors), then merged into maximal collinear runs; plus one unresolved floor and ceiling per zone.
/// Edges that are neither shared nor on the boundary indicate gaps or overlaps and are errors.
/// </summary>
public sealed class LayoutSurfaceBuilder
{
    private readonly ToleranceSettings _tolerances;

    /// <summary>Creates the builder.</summary>
    /// <param name="tolerances">Tolerances; <see cref="ToleranceSettings.Distance"/> is the vertex matching grid.</param>
    public LayoutSurfaceBuilder(ToleranceSettings tolerances)
    {
        _tolerances = tolerances;
    }

    /// <summary>Builds walls (without windows), floors, and ceilings.</summary>
    /// <param name="footprint">Plan boundary; walls on it are outdoors.</param>
    /// <param name="zones">Zones tiling the footprint.</param>
    /// <param name="height">Floor-to-floor height, m.</param>
    /// <returns>Surfaces grouped by zone (walls, floor, ceiling), or errors for gaps and overlaps.</returns>
    public Result<IReadOnlyList<Surface>> Build(Polygon2 footprint, IReadOnlyList<LayoutZone> zones, double height)
    {
        var grid = new VertexGrid(_tolerances.Distance);
        foreach (Point2 p in footprint.Rings.SelectMany(r => r).Concat(zones.SelectMany(z => z.Footprint.Rings.SelectMany(r => r))))
        {
            grid.Register(p);
        }

        GridPoint[] vertices = grid.Keys.OrderBy(k => k.X).ThenBy(k => k.Y).ToArray();
        var segments = new List<Segment>();
        for (int zoneIndex = 0; zoneIndex < zones.Count; zoneIndex++)
        {
            int ringIndex = 0;
            foreach (IReadOnlyList<Point2> ring in zones[zoneIndex].Footprint.Rings)
            {
                AddRingSegments(grid, vertices, zoneIndex, ringIndex++, ring, segments);
            }
        }

        var diagnostics = new List<Diagnostic>();
        Classify(grid, footprint, zones, segments, diagnostics);
        if (diagnostics.Count > 0)
        {
            return Result.Failure<IReadOnlyList<Surface>>(diagnostics);
        }

        var surfaces = new List<Surface>();
        for (int zoneIndex = 0; zoneIndex < zones.Count; zoneIndex++)
        {
            LayoutZone zone = zones[zoneIndex];
            int wallNumber = 1;
            foreach (IGrouping<int, Segment> ring in segments.Where(s => s.Zone == zoneIndex).GroupBy(s => s.Ring))
            {
                foreach (List<Segment> run in MergeRuns(ring.ToList()))
                {
                    Segment first = run[0];
                    surfaces.Add(new WallSurface(
                        new SurfaceId($"{zone.Id.Value}/W{wallNumber++}"),
                        zone.Id,
                        grid.Original(first.A),
                        grid.Original(run[run.Count - 1].B),
                        elevation: 0.0,
                        height,
                        first.Boundary,
                        first.Adjacent is int adjacent ? zones[adjacent].Id : null,
                        Array.Empty<Window>()));
                }
            }

            surfaces.Add(new HorizontalSurface(new SurfaceId($"{zone.Id.Value}/F"), zone.Id, HorizontalKind.Floor, zone.Footprint, 0.0, BoundaryCondition.Unresolved, null));
            surfaces.Add(new HorizontalSurface(new SurfaceId($"{zone.Id.Value}/C"), zone.Id, HorizontalKind.Ceiling, zone.Footprint, height, BoundaryCondition.Unresolved, null));
        }

        return Result.Success<IReadOnlyList<Surface>>(surfaces);
    }

    private static void AddRingSegments(VertexGrid grid, GridPoint[] vertices, int zone, int ring, IReadOnlyList<Point2> points, List<Segment> segments)
    {
        for (int i = 0; i < points.Count; i++)
        {
            GridPoint a = grid.Key(points[i]);
            GridPoint b = grid.Key(points[(i + 1) % points.Count]);
            if (a == b)
            {
                continue;
            }

            GridPoint[] cuts = vertices
                .Where(v => v != a && v != b && VertexGrid.IsStrictlyInside(a, b, v))
                .OrderBy(v => VertexGrid.Parameter(a, b, v))
                .ToArray();
            GridPoint previous = a;
            foreach (GridPoint cut in cuts.Concat(new[] { b }))
            {
                segments.Add(new Segment(zone, ring, previous, cut));
                previous = cut;
            }
        }
    }

    private static void Classify(VertexGrid grid, Polygon2 footprint, IReadOnlyList<LayoutZone> zones, List<Segment> segments, List<Diagnostic> diagnostics)
    {
        var byDirection = segments.GroupBy(s => (s.A, s.B)).ToDictionary(g => g.Key, g => g.ToList());
        GridPoint[][] boundaryRings = footprint.Rings.Select(r => r.Select(grid.Key).ToArray()).ToArray();
        foreach (Segment segment in segments)
        {
            string subject = zones[segment.Zone].Id.Value;
            if (byDirection[(segment.A, segment.B)].Count > 1)
            {
                diagnostics.Add(Diagnostic.Error(DiagnosticCodes.OverlappingZones, $"Edge {Describe(grid, segment)} is used by more than one zone in the same direction.", subject));
                continue;
            }

            if (byDirection.TryGetValue((segment.B, segment.A), out List<Segment>? opposite))
            {
                segment.Boundary = BoundaryCondition.Interzone;
                segment.Adjacent = opposite[0].Zone;
            }
            else if (boundaryRings.Any(ring => OnRing(ring, segment.A, segment.B)))
            {
                segment.Boundary = BoundaryCondition.Outdoors;
            }
            else
            {
                diagnostics.Add(Diagnostic.Error(DiagnosticCodes.UnmatchedEdge, $"Edge {Describe(grid, segment)} is neither shared with another zone nor on the plan boundary (gap or overlap).", subject));
            }
        }
    }

    private IEnumerable<List<Segment>> MergeRuns(List<Segment> ring)
    {
        int start = Enumerable.Range(0, ring.Count).FirstOrDefault(i => !CanMerge(ring[(i + ring.Count - 1) % ring.Count], ring[i]));
        List<Segment>? run = null;
        for (int k = 0; k < ring.Count; k++)
        {
            Segment segment = ring[(start + k) % ring.Count];
            if (run is not null && CanMerge(run[run.Count - 1], segment))
            {
                run.Add(segment);
                continue;
            }

            if (run is not null)
            {
                yield return run;
            }

            run = new List<Segment> { segment };
        }

        if (run is not null)
        {
            yield return run;
        }
    }

    private bool CanMerge(Segment previous, Segment next)
    {
        if (previous.B != next.A || previous.Boundary != next.Boundary || previous.Adjacent != next.Adjacent)
        {
            return false;
        }

        double ax = previous.B.X - previous.A.X;
        double ay = previous.B.Y - previous.A.Y;
        double bx = next.B.X - next.A.X;
        double by = next.B.Y - next.A.Y;
        double lengths = Math.Sqrt((ax * ax) + (ay * ay)) * Math.Sqrt((bx * bx) + (by * by));
        double sine = ((ax * by) - (ay * bx)) / lengths;
        double cosine = ((ax * bx) + (ay * by)) / lengths;
        return cosine > 0 && Math.Abs(sine) <= Math.Sin(_tolerances.Angle);
    }

    private static bool OnRing(GridPoint[] ring, GridPoint a, GridPoint b)
    {
        for (int i = 0; i < ring.Length; i++)
        {
            GridPoint p = ring[i];
            GridPoint q = ring[(i + 1) % ring.Length];
            if (VertexGrid.IsOnSegment(p, q, a) && VertexGrid.IsOnSegment(p, q, b))
            {
                return true;
            }
        }

        return false;
    }

    private static string Describe(VertexGrid grid, Segment segment)
    {
        Point2 a = grid.Original(segment.A);
        Point2 b = grid.Original(segment.B);
        return $"({a.X:0.###}, {a.Y:0.###})-({b.X:0.###}, {b.Y:0.###})";
    }

    private sealed class Segment
    {
        public Segment(int zone, int ring, GridPoint a, GridPoint b)
        {
            Zone = zone;
            Ring = ring;
            A = a;
            B = b;
        }

        public int Zone { get; }

        public int Ring { get; }

        public GridPoint A { get; }

        public GridPoint B { get; }

        public BoundaryCondition Boundary { get; set; } = BoundaryCondition.Unresolved;

        public int? Adjacent { get; set; }
    }
}
```

- [x] **Step 5: Union tiles — replace `src/Lod.Core/Geometry/PolygonOps.cs` (full file)**

```csharp
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.Linq;
using Clipper2Lib;
using Lod.Core.Common;

namespace Lod.Core.Geometry;

/// <summary>
/// Polygon boolean operations on Clipper2 (ADR-002). Results are snapped to the <see cref="ToleranceSettings.Distance"/> grid.
/// </summary>
public sealed class PolygonOps
{
    private readonly int _precision;
    private readonly double _distance;

    /// <summary>Creates the operations with the clipping precision of <paramref name="tolerances"/>.</summary>
    /// <param name="tolerances">Tolerances; <see cref="ToleranceSettings.DecimalPrecision"/> sets the snapping grid.</param>
    public PolygonOps(ToleranceSettings tolerances)
    {
        _precision = tolerances.DecimalPrecision;
        _distance = tolerances.Distance;
    }

    /// <summary>Intersection of two polygons.</summary>
    /// <param name="a">First polygon.</param>
    /// <param name="b">Second polygon.</param>
    /// <returns>Zero or more polygons.</returns>
    public IReadOnlyList<Polygon2> Intersect(Polygon2 a, Polygon2 b) => Execute(ClipType.Intersection, new[] { a }, new[] { b });

    /// <summary>Union of polygons.</summary>
    /// <param name="polygons">Polygons to merge.</param>
    /// <returns>Disjoint polygons covering the union.</returns>
    public IReadOnlyList<Polygon2> Union(IEnumerable<Polygon2> polygons) => Execute(ClipType.Union, polygons, Enumerable.Empty<Polygon2>());

    /// <summary>
    /// Union of polygons that tile a region and share their edges up to the distance tolerance, such as the zones of a storey (ADR-013). Before
    /// clipping, vertices in the same cell of the distance grid are unified, and every vertex lying on another tile's edge within one grid unit
    /// (a T-junction) is inserted into that edge, as <see cref="Layout.LayoutSurfaceBuilder"/> splits walls. Shared edges then cancel exactly;
    /// a plain <see cref="Union"/> of tiles that are not axis-aligned can leave slits or splits where rounding on the clipping grid moves a
    /// T-junction off its edge. Inserted vertices lie on their edges within the distance tolerance, so the outline is not moved.
    /// </summary>
    /// <param name="tiles">Polygons tiling a region.</param>
    /// <returns>Disjoint polygons covering the union.</returns>
    public IReadOnlyList<Polygon2> UnionTiles(IReadOnlyList<Polygon2> tiles)
    {
        var grid = new VertexGrid(_distance);
        foreach (Point2 p in tiles.SelectMany(t => t.Rings).SelectMany(r => r))
        {
            grid.Register(p);
        }

        GridPoint[] vertices = grid.Keys.OrderBy(k => k.X).ThenBy(k => k.Y).ToArray();
        IEnumerable<Point2> Split(IReadOnlyList<Point2> ring)
        {
            for (int i = 0; i < ring.Count; i++)
            {
                GridPoint a = grid.Key(ring[i]);
                GridPoint b = grid.Key(ring[(i + 1) % ring.Count]);
                yield return grid.Original(a);
                if (a == b)
                {
                    continue;
                }

                foreach (GridPoint cut in vertices.Where(v => v != a && v != b && VertexGrid.IsStrictlyInside(a, b, v)).OrderBy(v => VertexGrid.Parameter(a, b, v)))
                {
                    yield return grid.Original(cut);
                }
            }
        }

        // A tile whose rings collapse on the grid cannot be split; it is clipped as given.
        Polygon2[] prepared = tiles
            .Select(t => Polygon2.Create(Split(t.Outer).ToArray(), t.Holes.Select(h => Split(h).ToArray()).ToArray()) is { IsSuccess: true } split ? split.Value : t)
            .ToArray();
        return Union(prepared);
    }

    /// <summary>Part of <paramref name="subject"/> not covered by <paramref name="clips"/>.</summary>
    /// <param name="subject">Polygon to cut.</param>
    /// <param name="clips">Polygons to remove.</param>
    /// <returns>Zero or more polygons.</returns>
    public IReadOnlyList<Polygon2> Difference(Polygon2 subject, IEnumerable<Polygon2> clips) => Execute(ClipType.Difference, new[] { subject }, clips);

    /// <summary>Area of the intersection of two polygons.</summary>
    /// <param name="a">First polygon.</param>
    /// <param name="b">Second polygon.</param>
    /// <returns>Overlap area, m².</returns>
    public double IntersectionArea(Polygon2 a, Polygon2 b) => Intersect(a, b).Sum(p => p.Area);

    private IReadOnlyList<Polygon2> Execute(ClipType clipType, IEnumerable<Polygon2> subjects, IEnumerable<Polygon2> clips)
    {
        var clipper = new ClipperD(_precision);
        clipper.AddSubject(ToPaths(subjects));
        clipper.AddClip(ToPaths(clips));
        var tree = new PolyTreeD();
        clipper.Execute(clipType, FillRule.NonZero, tree);
        var result = new List<Polygon2>();
        Collect(tree, result);
        return result;
    }

    private static PathsD ToPaths(IEnumerable<Polygon2> polygons)
    {
        var paths = new PathsD();
        foreach (IReadOnlyList<Point2> ring in polygons.SelectMany(p => p.Rings))
        {
            var path = new PathD(ring.Count);
            path.AddRange(ring.Select(p => new PointD(p.X, p.Y)));
            paths.Add(path);
        }

        return paths;
    }

    private static void Collect(PolyPathD parent, List<Polygon2> result)
    {
        for (int i = 0; i < parent.Count; i++)
        {
            PolyPathD outer = parent[i];
            var holes = new List<IEnumerable<Point2>>();
            for (int j = 0; j < outer.Count; j++)
            {
                PolyPathD hole = outer[j];
                holes.Add(ToPoints(hole.Polygon!));
                Collect(hole, result);
            }

            Result<Polygon2> polygon = Polygon2.Create(ToPoints(outer.Polygon!), holes);
            if (polygon.IsSuccess)
            {
                result.Add(polygon.Value);
            }
        }
    }

    private static IEnumerable<Point2> ToPoints(PathD path) => path.Select(p => new Point2(p.x, p.y));
}
```

```csharp
using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.Linq;
using Lod.Core.Aggregation;
using Lod.Core.Common;
using Lod.Core.Geometry;
using Lod.Core.Layout;
using Lod.Core.Model;
using Lod.Core.Programs;
using Lod.Core.Simplification;

namespace Lod.Core.Buildings;

/// <summary>A zone that a single-zone method merges storeys into.</summary>
/// <param name="Id">Zone ID.</param>
/// <param name="Name">Display name.</param>
internal sealed record MergedZone(ZoneId Id, string Name);

/// <summary>
/// The merge shared by the single-zone methods (ADR-013): the storeys of the assembled stack are grouped by floor entry into zones. Each zone gets one
/// part per storey outline (the union of the storey's zone footprints as tiles, <see cref="PolygonOps.UnionTiles"/>); its outdoor walls are rebuilt per outline, must cover every source façade (D-041), and keep every source window in
/// place (D-039); walls between merged zones are discarded (D-034); slabs between two storeys of the same zone become internal mass with both faces
/// exposed, one entry per slab level (D-031); every other floor and ceiling piece keeps its stacked designation (D-068), and a piece between two
/// zones stays interzone. Programs are aggregated from the stacked zones with fractions 1, so every invariant is conserved (ADR-005, ADR-007).
/// </summary>
internal static class SingleZoneMerge
{
    /// <summary>Merges the stacked storeys of <paramref name="floors"/> into the zones given per floor entry.</summary>
    /// <param name="operation">Operation name recorded in provenance.</param>
    /// <param name="floors">Floor entries, bottom to top.</param>
    /// <param name="zoneOfEntry">The zone each floor entry's storeys merge into; equal zones are merged together.</param>
    /// <param name="tolerances">Tolerances.</param>
    /// <returns>The building, or errors.</returns>
    public static Result<IGeneratedBuilding> Merge(string operation, IReadOnlyList<FloorEntry> floors, Func<int, MergedZone> zoneOfEntry, ToleranceSettings tolerances)
    {
        Result<AssembledStack> assembled = Storeys.Assemble(floors, tolerances);
        if (!assembled.IsSuccess)
        {
            return Result.Failure<IGeneratedBuilding>(assembled.Diagnostics);
        }

        AssembledStack stack = assembled.Value;
        MergedZone[] zoneOfStorey = stack.Entries.Select(zoneOfEntry).ToArray();
        MergedZone[] targets = zoneOfStorey.Distinct().ToArray();
        Dictionary<ZoneId, ZoneId> targetOf = stack.Storeys
            .SelectMany((s, k) => s.Zones.Select(z => (Source: z.Id, Target: zoneOfStorey[k].Id)))
            .ToDictionary(p => p.Source, p => p.Target);
        var ops = new PolygonOps(tolerances);
        var builder = new LayoutSurfaceBuilder(tolerances);
        Dictionary<ZoneId, List<ZonePart>> parts = targets.ToDictionary(t => t.Id, _ => new List<ZonePart>());
        var walls = new List<Surface>();
        for (int k = 0; k < stack.Storeys.Count; k++)
        {
            PlacedStorey storey = stack.Storeys[k];
            ZoneId target = zoneOfStorey[k].Id;
            foreach (Polygon2 outline in ops.UnionTiles(storey.Zones.Select(z => z.Parts[0].Footprint).ToArray()))
            {
                parts[target].Add(new ZonePart(outline, storey.Elevation, storey.Height));
                Result<IReadOnlyList<Surface>> built = builder.Build(outline, new[] { new LayoutZone(target, outline) }, storey.Height);
                if (!built.IsSuccess)
                {
                    return Result.Failure<IGeneratedBuilding>(built.Diagnostics);
                }

                string prefix = $"S{parts[target].Count - 1}/";
                walls.AddRange(built.Value.OfType<WallSurface>().Select(w => w.Relocate(id => id, prefix, storey.Elevation)));
            }
        }

        WallSurface[] sourceWalls = stack.Storeys.SelectMany(s => s.Walls).ToArray();
        FacadeAttribution facade = FacadeAttribution.Compute(sourceWalls, walls, tolerances);
        Diagnostic[] gaps = facade.CoverageGaps()
            .Select(g => Diagnostic.Error(DiagnosticCodes.FacadeNotCovered, $"Rebuilt façades cover {g.CoveredLength:0.######} m of this {g.Wall.Length:0.######} m wall (D-041).", g.Wall.Id.Value))
            .ToArray();
        if (gaps.Length > 0)
        {
            return Result.Failure<IGeneratedBuilding>(gaps);
        }

        Result<IReadOnlyList<Surface>> rehosted = WindowRehosting.Rehost(sourceWalls, walls, tolerances);
        if (!rehosted.IsSuccess)
        {
            return Result.Failure<IGeneratedBuilding>(rehosted.Diagnostics);
        }

        IReadOnlyList<Surface> glazedWalls = rehosted.Value;
        HorizontalSurface[] horizontal = stack.Horizontal.SelectMany(h => h).ToArray();
        bool Within(HorizontalSurface h) => h.Boundary == BoundaryCondition.Interzone && targetOf[h.Zone] == targetOf[h.AdjacentZone!.Value];
        InternalMass[] masses = targets
            .SelectMany(t => horizontal
                .Where(h => h.Kind == HorizontalKind.Floor && Within(h) && targetOf[h.Zone] == t.Id)
                .GroupBy(h => h.Elevation)
                .OrderBy(g => g.Key)
                .Select(level => new InternalMass(
                    t.Id,
                    level.Sum(h => h.Area),
                    ExposedFaces: 2,
                    level.Key,
                    level.Select(h => h.Id)
                        .Concat(horizontal.Where(c => c.Kind == HorizontalKind.Ceiling && Within(c) && targetOf[c.Zone] == t.Id && c.Elevation == level.Key).Select(c => c.Id))
                        .ToArray())))
            .ToArray();
        IEnumerable<Surface> envelope = horizontal
            .Where(h => !Within(h))
            .Select(h => new HorizontalSurface(h.Id, targetOf[h.Zone], h.Kind, h.Polygon, h.Elevation, h.Boundary, h.AdjacentZone is { } adjacent ? targetOf[adjacent] : null));

        var diagnostics = new List<Diagnostic>(assembled.Diagnostics);
        var aggregator = new EquivalentPropertyAggregator(tolerances);
        var zones = new List<Zone>();
        foreach (MergedZone target in targets)
        {
            Zone[] sources = stack.Storeys.Where((_, k) => zoneOfStorey[k] == target).SelectMany(s => s.Zones).ToArray();
            List<ZonePart> zoneParts = parts[target.Id];
            var measures = new ZoneMeasures(
                zoneParts.Sum(p => p.Footprint.Area),
                zoneParts.Sum(p => p.Volume),
                glazedWalls.Where(w => w.Zone == target.Id).Sum(w => w.Area));
            SourceZoneContribution[] contributions = sources
                .Select(z => new SourceZoneContribution(z.Id, z.Program, LayoutMeasures.Of(z, sourceWalls), 1.0, 1.0))
                .ToArray();
            Result<ZoneProgram> program = aggregator.Aggregate(target.Id, measures, contributions);
            diagnostics.AddRange(program.Diagnostics);
            if (program.IsSuccess)
            {
                SpaceType[] types = sources.Select(z => z.SpaceType).Distinct().ToArray();
                zones.Add(new Zone(target.Id, target.Name, types.Length == 1 ? types[0] : SpaceType.Mixed, zoneParts, program.Value, sources.Select(z => z.Id)));
            }
        }

        if (diagnostics.Any(d => d.Severity == DiagnosticSeverity.Error))
        {
            return Result.Failure<IGeneratedBuilding>(diagnostics);
        }

        var provenance = Provenance.Of(
            operation,
            new[] { new KeyValuePair<string, string>("Multipliers", string.Join(",", floors.Select(e => e.Multiplier))) },
            floors.Select(e => e.Floor.Provenance).ToArray());
        IGeneratedBuilding building = new GeneratedBuilding(floors[0].Floor.OrientationDegrees, zones, glazedWalls.Concat(envelope), masses, floors, provenance);
        return Result.Success(building, diagnostics);
    }
}
```

- [x] **Step 1: Write the failing test — replace `tests/Lod.Integration.Tests/PlanTransformTests.cs` (full file)**

The fact `RoundingSliversOfARotatedSetbackDoNotMakeAStoreyUnsupported` is added at the end: *Perimeter Core* floors of the 24 m plan × 3 and the 14 m plan × 4, both rotated by 55.27° and moved by (3.1, −7.7), leave between 1e-6 and 1e-4 m² of the setback storey's floor uncovered (grid rounding along shared façades). No storey is reported as unsupported; the zone multiplier still splits the setback storey off (`L0x1,L1x1,L2x1,L3x1,L5x2,L6x1`), and its building validates.

```csharp
using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.Linq;
using Lod.Core.Buildings;
using Lod.Core.Common;
using Lod.Core.Geometry;
using Lod.Core.Model;
using Lod.Core.Plans;
using Lod.Core.Simplification;
using Lod.Core.Validation;
using Xunit;

namespace Lod.Integration.Tests;

public sealed class PlanTransformTests
{
    [Theory]
    [InlineData(0.0)]
    [InlineData(90.0)]
    [InlineData(30.0)]
    public void RotationKeepsAreaVolumeWallAreaAndGlazing(double degrees)
    {
        IGeneratedPlan plan = Pipeline.Plan();

        IGeneratedPlan moved = PlanTransform.Apply(plan, degrees, 5.3, -2.1).Value;
        Totals before = Totals.Of(plan.Zones, plan.Surfaces, plan.OrientationDegrees);
        Totals after = Totals.Of(moved.Zones, moved.Surfaces, moved.OrientationDegrees);

        Assert.True(Pipeline.Tolerances.AreaEquals(plan.Footprint.Area, moved.Footprint.Area));
        Assert.True(Pipeline.Tolerances.AreaEquals(before.FloorArea, after.FloorArea));
        Assert.True(Pipeline.Tolerances.AreaEquals(before.Volume, after.Volume));
        Assert.True(Pipeline.Tolerances.AreaEquals(before.ExteriorWallArea, after.ExteriorWallArea));
        Assert.True(Pipeline.Tolerances.AreaEquals(before.Glazing, after.Glazing));
        Assert.Equal(plan.Zones.Select(z => z.Id), moved.Zones.Select(z => z.Id));
        Assert.Equal(plan.Surfaces.Select(s => s.Id), moved.Surfaces.Select(s => s.Id));
        Assert.Equal(plan.OrientationDegrees, moved.OrientationDegrees);
        Assert.Equal(plan.FloorHeight, moved.FloorHeight);
    }

    [Fact]
    public void GlazingPerOrientationFollowsTheRotatedFacades()
    {
        IGeneratedPlan plan = Pipeline.Plan();
        IReadOnlyDictionary<OrientationBin, double> before = Totals.Of(plan.Zones, plan.Surfaces, plan.OrientationDegrees).GlazingByOrientation;

        IGeneratedPlan quarter = PlanTransform.Apply(plan, 90.0, 0.0, 0.0).Value;
        IGeneratedPlan thirty = PlanTransform.Apply(plan, 30.0, 0.0, 0.0).Value;
        IReadOnlyDictionary<OrientationBin, double> afterQuarter = Totals.Of(quarter.Zones, quarter.Surfaces, quarter.OrientationDegrees).GlazingByOrientation;
        IReadOnlyDictionary<OrientationBin, double> afterThirty = Totals.Of(thirty.Zones, thirty.Surfaces, thirty.OrientationDegrees).GlazingByOrientation;

        // A quarter turn counter-clockwise turns a north façade west, east north, south east, and west south.
        Assert.Equal(before[OrientationBin.North], afterQuarter[OrientationBin.West], 9);
        Assert.Equal(before[OrientationBin.East], afterQuarter[OrientationBin.North], 9);
        Assert.Equal(before[OrientationBin.South], afterQuarter[OrientationBin.East], 9);
        Assert.Equal(before[OrientationBin.West], afterQuarter[OrientationBin.South], 9);

        // 30° keeps every façade azimuth inside its 90° bin (0° → 330°, 90° → 60°, ...).
        Assert.All(before.Keys, bin => Assert.Equal(before[bin], afterThirty[bin], 9));
    }

    [Fact]
    public void WallFacingIsRecomputedFromTheRotatedGeometry()
    {
        IGeneratedPlan plan = Pipeline.Plan();

        IGeneratedPlan moved = PlanTransform.Apply(plan, 90.0, 0.0, 0.0).Value;

        WallSurface[] original = plan.Surfaces.OfType<WallSurface>().ToArray();
        WallSurface[] rotated = moved.Surfaces.OfType<WallSurface>().ToArray();
        Assert.Equal(original.Length, rotated.Length);
        for (int i = 0; i < original.Length; i++)
        {
            Assert.Equal(Orientation.Normalise(original[i].PlanAzimuth - 90.0), rotated[i].PlanAzimuth, 9);
            Assert.Equal(original[i].Windows.Select(w => (w.Offset, w.SillHeight, w.Width, w.Height)), rotated[i].Windows.Select(w => (w.Offset, w.SillHeight, w.Width, w.Height)));
        }

        WallSurface northFacing = original.First(w => w.Boundary == BoundaryCondition.Outdoors && Orientation.Bin(w.PlanAzimuth) == OrientationBin.North);
        Assert.Equal(OrientationBin.West, Orientation.Bin(rotated[Array.IndexOf(original, northFacing)].PlanAzimuth));
    }

    [Fact]
    public void TranslationOnlyMovesEveryPoint()
    {
        IGeneratedPlan plan = Pipeline.Plan();

        IGeneratedPlan moved = PlanTransform.Apply(plan, 0.0, 5.3, -2.1).Value;

        Assert.Equal(plan.Footprint.Outer.Select(p => new Point2(p.X + 5.3, p.Y - 2.1)), moved.Footprint.Outer);
        Assert.Equal(
            plan.Zones.SelectMany(z => z.Parts).SelectMany(p => p.Footprint.Outer).Select(p => new Point2(p.X + 5.3, p.Y - 2.1)),
            moved.Zones.SelectMany(z => z.Parts).SelectMany(p => p.Footprint.Outer));
        Assert.Equal(
            plan.Surfaces.OfType<WallSurface>().Select(w => (new Point2(w.Start.X + 5.3, w.Start.Y - 2.1), new Point2(w.End.X + 5.3, w.End.Y - 2.1), w.Elevation, w.Height)),
            moved.Surfaces.OfType<WallSurface>().Select(w => (w.Start, w.End, w.Elevation, w.Height)));
        Assert.Equal(
            plan.Surfaces.OfType<HorizontalSurface>().SelectMany(h => h.Polygon.Outer).Select(p => new Point2(p.X + 5.3, p.Y - 2.1)),
            moved.Surfaces.OfType<HorizontalSurface>().SelectMany(h => h.Polygon.Outer));
        Assert.All(moved.Surfaces.OfType<HorizontalSurface>(), h => Assert.Equal(BoundaryCondition.Unresolved, h.Boundary));
    }

    [Fact]
    public void TransformIsDeterministicAndRecordedInProvenance()
    {
        IGeneratedPlan plan = Pipeline.Plan();

        IGeneratedPlan first = PlanTransform.Apply(plan, 30.0, 5.3, -2.1).Value;
        IGeneratedPlan second = PlanTransform.Apply(plan, 30.0, 5.3, -2.1).Value;

        Assert.Equal(TextReport.Describe(first), TextReport.Describe(second));
        Assert.Equal("TransformPlan", first.Provenance.Operation);
        Assert.Equal(
            new[] { "RotationDegrees=30", "TranslationX=5.3", "TranslationY=-2.1" },
            first.Provenance.Parameters.Select(p => $"{p.Key}={p.Value}"));
        Assert.Same(plan.Provenance, Assert.Single(first.Provenance.Inputs));
    }

    [Theory]
    [InlineData(double.NaN, 0.0, 0.0)]
    [InlineData(0.0, double.PositiveInfinity, 0.0)]
    [InlineData(0.0, 0.0, double.NegativeInfinity)]
    [InlineData(double.PositiveInfinity, double.NaN, double.NaN)]
    public void NonFiniteInputsAreInvalidParameters(double degrees, double x, double y)
    {
        Result<IGeneratedPlan> result = PlanTransform.Apply(Pipeline.Plan(), degrees, x, y);

        Assert.False(result.IsSuccess);
        Assert.All(result.Diagnostics, d => Assert.Equal(DiagnosticCodes.InvalidParameter, d.Code));
        Assert.Equal(new[] { degrees, x, y }.Count(v => double.IsNaN(v) || double.IsInfinity(v)), result.Diagnostics.Count);
    }

    public static IEnumerable<object[]> Matrix() =>
        from placement in new[] { (30.0, 5.3, -2.1), (7.3, 0.0, 0.0), (131.4, 15.08, -153.9), (248.2, 42.62, -245.2) }
        from simplifier in new[] { "NoSimplification", "SemanticMerge", "PerimeterCore", "SingleZonePerFloor" }
        from aggregator in new[] { "Stack", "StackedFloorZoneMultiplier", "SingleZonePerFloorType", "SingleZoneBuilding" }
        select new object[] { placement.Item1, placement.Item2, placement.Item3, simplifier, aggregator };

    [Theory]
    [MemberData(nameof(Matrix))]
    public void EveryCombinationValidatesOnARotatedAndTranslatedPlan(double degrees, double x, double y, string simplifier, string aggregator)
    {
        IGeneratedPlan plan = PlanTransform.Apply(Pipeline.Plan(), degrees, x, y).Value;
        Result<IFloor> floor = Pipeline.Simplifier(simplifier).Simplify(plan);
        Assert.True(floor.IsSuccess, string.Join("; ", floor.Diagnostics));
        FloorEntry[] entries = { new(floor.Value, 1), new(floor.Value, 3), new(floor.Value, 1) };

        Result<IGeneratedBuilding> building = Pipeline.Aggregator(aggregator).Aggregate(entries);
        Assert.True(building.IsSuccess, string.Join("; ", building.Diagnostics));
        ValidationReport report = new BuildingValidator(Pipeline.Tolerances).Validate(building.Value);

        Assert.True(report.Passed, report.Describe());
        Assert.DoesNotContain(building.Diagnostics, d => d.Severity == DiagnosticSeverity.Warning);
    }

    [Fact]
    public void RoundingSliversOfARotatedSetbackDoNotMakeAStoreyUnsupported()
    {
        // Rotated by 55.27°, the perimeter zones of the 14 m plan leave about 1.4e-5 m² of its floor uncovered by the 24 m plan below, from rounding
        // on the distance grid along their shared façades. Compared with its 252 m² floor that is within the area tolerance: the setback storey is
        // supported (D-074). The zone multiplier still splits it off, so even the sliver is not multiplied (D-046), and the building validates.
        IFloor wide = new PerimeterCore(Pipeline.Tolerances, 4.57).Simplify(PlanTransform.Apply(Pipeline.Plan(), 55.27, 3.1, -7.7).Value).Value;
        IFloor narrow = new PerimeterCore(Pipeline.Tolerances, 4.57).Simplify(PlanTransform.Apply(Pipeline.Plan(Pipeline.Canonical with { Length = 14.0 }), 55.27, 3.1, -7.7).Value).Value;
        FloorEntry[] entries = { new(wide, 3), new(narrow, 4) };

        Result<IGeneratedBuilding> stacked = new Stack(Pipeline.Tolerances).Aggregate(entries);
        Result<IGeneratedBuilding> multiplied = new StackedFloorZoneMultiplier(Pipeline.Tolerances).Aggregate(entries);

        Assert.InRange(Totals.Of(stacked.Value.Zones, stacked.Value.Surfaces, stacked.Value.OrientationDegrees).ExposedFloorArea, 1e-6, 1e-4);
        Assert.DoesNotContain(stacked.Diagnostics, d => d.Code == DiagnosticCodes.UnsupportedStorey);
        Assert.DoesNotContain(multiplied.Diagnostics, d => d.Code == DiagnosticCodes.UnsupportedStorey);
        Assert.Equal("L0x1,L1x1,L2x1,L3x1,L5x2,L6x1", multiplied.Value.Provenance.Parameters.Single(p => p.Key == "Storeys").Value);
        ValidationReport report = new BuildingValidator(Pipeline.Tolerances).Validate(multiplied.Value);
        Assert.True(report.Passed, report.Describe());
    }

    [Theory]
    [InlineData("Stack")]
    [InlineData("StackedFloorZoneMultiplier")]
    [InlineData("SingleZonePerFloorType")]
    [InlineData("SingleZoneBuilding")]
    public void AStoreyMovedOffTheStoreyBelowIsUnsupported(string aggregator)
    {
        // The upper plan is moved by (5.3, -2.1): it overlaps the lower 24 m x 18 m plan on 18.7 m x 15.9 m = 297.33 m², so 134.67 m² overhang.
        IFloor lower = new NoSimplification().Simplify(Pipeline.Plan()).Value;
        IFloor upper = new NoSimplification().Simplify(PlanTransform.Apply(Pipeline.Plan(), 0.0, 5.3, -2.1).Value).Value;

        Result<IGeneratedBuilding> result = Pipeline.Aggregator(aggregator).Aggregate(new[] { new FloorEntry(lower, 2), new FloorEntry(upper, 2) });

        Diagnostic warning = Assert.Single(result.Diagnostics, d => d.Code == DiagnosticCodes.UnsupportedStorey);
        Assert.Equal(DiagnosticSeverity.Warning, warning.Severity);
        Assert.Equal("L2", warning.Subject);
        Assert.Contains("134.670000 m²", warning.Message);
        ValidationReport report = new BuildingValidator(Pipeline.Tolerances).Validate(result.Value);
        Assert.True(report.Passed, report.Describe());
        Totals totals = Totals.Of(result.Value.Zones, result.Value.Surfaces, result.Value.OrientationDegrees);
        Assert.Equal(134.67, totals.ExposedFloorArea, 6);
        Assert.Equal(432.0 + 134.67, totals.RoofArea, 6);
        Assert.Equal(432.0, totals.GroundArea, 6);
    }
}
```

```csharp
using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.Linq;
using Lod.Core.Common;
using Lod.Core.Geometry;
using Lod.Core.Model;

namespace Lod.Core.Buildings;

/// <summary>A floor placed at its elevation with storey-prefixed zone and surface IDs.</summary>
/// <param name="Index">Index of the storey in the building, from 0 at the bottom.</param>
/// <param name="Elevation">Floor elevation, m.</param>
/// <param name="Height">Floor-to-floor height, m.</param>
/// <param name="Zones">Relocated zones.</param>
/// <param name="Walls">Relocated walls.</param>
public sealed record PlacedStorey(int Index, double Elevation, double Height, IReadOnlyList<Zone> Zones, IReadOnlyList<WallSurface> Walls);

/// <summary>
/// Every storey of a building with every floor and ceiling designated on the whole stack (D-068): the input of every floor aggregator.
/// </summary>
public sealed class AssembledStack
{
    /// <summary>Creates the stack.</summary>
    /// <param name="storeys">Every storey, bottom to top, with multiplier 1.</param>
    /// <param name="entries">Index of the floor entry each storey belongs to.</param>
    /// <param name="horizontal">Floors and ceilings of each storey.</param>
    public AssembledStack(IReadOnlyList<PlacedStorey> storeys, IReadOnlyList<int> entries, IReadOnlyList<IReadOnlyList<HorizontalSurface>> horizontal)
    {
        Storeys = storeys;
        Entries = entries;
        Horizontal = horizontal;
    }

    /// <summary>Every storey, bottom to top; storey <c>k</c> has the ID prefix <c>L{k}/</c>.</summary>
    public IReadOnlyList<PlacedStorey> Storeys { get; }

    /// <summary>Index of the floor entry each storey belongs to.</summary>
    public IReadOnlyList<int> Entries { get; }

    /// <summary>Floors and ceilings of each storey: ground, roof (outdoors), exposed (outdoors), or interzone between two storeys.</summary>
    public IReadOnlyList<IReadOnlyList<HorizontalSurface>> Horizontal { get; }

    /// <summary>Area of a storey's floor or ceiling pieces that do not lie between two storeys (ground, roof, or exposed), m².</summary>
    /// <param name="storey">Storey index.</param>
    /// <param name="kind">Floor or ceiling.</param>
    /// <returns>The area.</returns>
    public double ExposedArea(int storey, HorizontalKind kind) =>
        Horizontal[storey].Where(h => h.Kind == kind && h.Boundary != BoundaryCondition.Interzone).Sum(h => h.Area);
}

/// <summary>Shared steps of the floor aggregators: input validation, vertical placement, and assembling and designating the stack.</summary>
public static class Storeys
{
    /// <summary>Checks that there is at least one floor, multipliers are positive, and all floors share one orientation.</summary>
    /// <param name="floors">Floor entries.</param>
    /// <param name="tolerances">Tolerances.</param>
    /// <returns>Errors, if any.</returns>
    public static IReadOnlyList<Diagnostic> Validate(IReadOnlyList<FloorEntry> floors, ToleranceSettings tolerances)
    {
        var diagnostics = new List<Diagnostic>();
        if (floors.Count == 0)
        {
            diagnostics.Add(Diagnostic.Error(DiagnosticCodes.NoFloors, "A building needs at least one floor."));
            return diagnostics;
        }

        foreach (FloorEntry entry in floors.Where(e => e.Multiplier < 1))
        {
            diagnostics.Add(Diagnostic.Error(DiagnosticCodes.InvalidParameter, $"Multipliers start at 1, got {entry.Multiplier}."));
        }

        double orientation = floors[0].Floor.OrientationDegrees;
        double toleranceDegrees = tolerances.Angle * 180.0 / Math.PI;
        if (floors.Any(e => Math.Abs(e.Floor.OrientationDegrees - orientation) > toleranceDegrees))
        {
            diagnostics.Add(Diagnostic.Error(DiagnosticCodes.OrientationMismatch, "All floors of a building must have the same orientation."));
        }

        return diagnostics;
    }

    /// <summary>Stacks floors bottom to top, one storey each, prefixing IDs with <c>L{index}/</c>.</summary>
    /// <param name="storeys">Floors in vertical order with the zone multiplier to apply.</param>
    /// <returns>The placed storeys.</returns>
    public static IReadOnlyList<PlacedStorey> Place(IEnumerable<(IFloor Floor, int ZoneMultiplier)> storeys)
    {
        var placed = new List<PlacedStorey>();
        double elevation = 0.0;
        foreach ((IFloor floor, int multiplier) in storeys)
        {
            placed.Add(PlaceAt(floor, placed.Count, elevation, multiplier));
            elevation += floor.FloorHeight;
        }

        return placed;
    }

    /// <summary>Places a floor as storey <paramref name="index"/> of the building, prefixing IDs with <c>L{index}/</c>.</summary>
    /// <param name="floor">The floor.</param>
    /// <param name="index">Storey index in the building, from 0 at the bottom.</param>
    /// <param name="elevation">Elevation of the storey, m.</param>
    /// <param name="zoneMultiplier">Zone multiplier to apply.</param>
    /// <returns>The placed storey.</returns>
    public static PlacedStorey PlaceAt(IFloor floor, int index, double elevation, int zoneMultiplier)
    {
        string prefix = $"L{index}/";
        ZoneId Rename(ZoneId id) => new(prefix + id.Value);
        Zone[] zones = floor.Zones.Select(z => z.Relocate(Rename(z.Id), elevation, zoneMultiplier)).ToArray();
        WallSurface[] walls = floor.Surfaces.OfType<WallSurface>().Select(w => w.Relocate(Rename, prefix, elevation)).ToArray();
        return new PlacedStorey(index, elevation, floor.FloorHeight, zones, walls);
    }

    /// <summary>
    /// Assembles every storey of a building (D-068): each entry repeated by its multiplier, bottom to top, at its floor-to-floor height, with every
    /// floor and ceiling designated on the whole stack. Reports a <see cref="DiagnosticCodes.UnsupportedStorey"/> warning for every storey whose
    /// floor area covered by the storey below differs from its floor area by more than the relative area tolerance (D-074).
    /// </summary>
    /// <param name="floors">Floor entries, bottom to top.</param>
    /// <param name="tolerances">Tolerances.</param>
    /// <returns>The assembled stack, or the errors of <see cref="Validate"/>.</returns>
    public static Result<AssembledStack> Assemble(IReadOnlyList<FloorEntry> floors, ToleranceSettings tolerances)
    {
        IReadOnlyList<Diagnostic> errors = Validate(floors, tolerances);
        if (errors.Count > 0)
        {
            return Result.Failure<AssembledStack>(errors);
        }

        int[] entries = floors.SelectMany((e, i) => Enumerable.Repeat(i, e.Multiplier)).ToArray();
        IReadOnlyList<PlacedStorey> storeys = Place(entries.Select(i => (floors[i].Floor, 1)));
        var ops = new PolygonOps(tolerances);
        IReadOnlyList<HorizontalSurface>[] horizontal = Enumerable.Range(0, storeys.Count).Select(k => ResolveStacked(storeys, k, ops)).ToArray();
        var stack = new AssembledStack(storeys, entries, horizontal);
        var warnings = new List<Diagnostic>();
        for (int k = 1; k < storeys.Count; k++)
        {
            double floorArea = storeys[k].Zones.Sum(z => z.FloorArea);
            double uncovered = stack.ExposedArea(k, HorizontalKind.Floor);
            if (!tolerances.AreaEquals(floorArea, floorArea - uncovered))
            {
                warnings.Add(Diagnostic.Warning(
                    DiagnosticCodes.UnsupportedStorey,
                    $"The floor of storey {k} is not fully covered by storey {k - 1}: {TextReport.F(uncovered)} m² of it faces outdoors (D-074).",
                    $"L{k}"));
            }
        }

        return Result.Success(stack, warnings);
    }

    /// <summary>
    /// Floors and ceilings of storey <paramref name="index"/> of physically stacked storeys: split by overlap with the zones below and above into
    /// interzone pieces; uncovered parts face outdoors; the lowest floors face the ground and the highest ceilings are roofs.
    /// </summary>
    /// <param name="storeys">Placed storeys, bottom to top.</param>
    /// <param name="index">Index of the storey to resolve.</param>
    /// <param name="ops">Polygon operations.</param>
    /// <returns>The storey's horizontal surfaces, zone by zone, floor pieces before ceiling pieces.</returns>
    public static IReadOnlyList<HorizontalSurface> ResolveStacked(IReadOnlyList<PlacedStorey> storeys, int index, PolygonOps ops)
    {
        var surfaces = new List<HorizontalSurface>();
        PlacedStorey storey = storeys[index];
        PlacedStorey? below = index > 0 ? storeys[index - 1] : null;
        PlacedStorey? above = index < storeys.Count - 1 ? storeys[index + 1] : null;
        foreach (Zone zone in storey.Zones)
        {
            Polygon2 footprint = zone.Parts[0].Footprint;
            surfaces.AddRange(Split(zone.Id, HorizontalKind.Floor, footprint, storey.Elevation, below, BoundaryCondition.Ground, ops));
            surfaces.AddRange(Split(zone.Id, HorizontalKind.Ceiling, footprint, storey.Elevation + storey.Height, above, BoundaryCondition.Outdoors, ops));
        }

        return surfaces;
    }

    private static IEnumerable<HorizontalSurface> Split(
        ZoneId zone,
        HorizontalKind kind,
        Polygon2 footprint,
        double elevation,
        PlacedStorey? neighbour,
        BoundaryCondition exposed,
        PolygonOps ops)
    {
        string tag = kind == HorizontalKind.Floor ? "F" : "C";
        if (neighbour is null)
        {
            yield return new HorizontalSurface(new SurfaceId($"{zone.Value}/{tag}1"), zone, kind, footprint, elevation, exposed, null);
            yield break;
        }

        int number = 1;
        foreach (Zone other in neighbour.Zones)
        {
            foreach (Polygon2 piece in ops.Intersect(footprint, other.Parts[0].Footprint))
            {
                yield return new HorizontalSurface(new SurfaceId($"{zone.Value}/{tag}{number++}"), zone, kind, piece, elevation, BoundaryCondition.Interzone, other.Id);
            }
        }

        foreach (Polygon2 piece in ops.Difference(footprint, neighbour.Zones.Select(z => z.Parts[0].Footprint)))
        {
            yield return new HorizontalSurface(new SurfaceId($"{zone.Value}/{tag}{number++}"), zone, kind, piece, elevation, BoundaryCondition.Outdoors, null);
        }
    }
}
```

```csharp
using Lod.Core.Common;

namespace Lod.Core.Tests.Conversion;

    private static readonly ZoneId B = new("B");

    private static readonly WallSurface InterzoneWall =
        new(new SurfaceId("A/W1"), A, new Point2(0, 0), new Point2(10, 0), 0.0, 3.0, BoundaryCondition.Interzone, B, Array.Empty<Window>());

    private static readonly WallSurface OutdoorWall =
        new(new SurfaceId("A/W2"), A, new Point2(10, 0), new Point2(10, 5), 0.0, 3.0, BoundaryCondition.Outdoors, null, Array.Empty<Window>());

    private static readonly HorizontalSurface InterzoneFloor =
        new(new SurfaceId("A/F1"), A, HorizontalKind.Floor, Polygon2.Rectangle(0, 0, 10, 5), 3.0, BoundaryCondition.Interzone, B);

    private static readonly HorizontalSurface InterzoneCeiling =
        new(new SurfaceId("A/C1"), A, HorizontalKind.Ceiling, Polygon2.Rectangle(0, 0, 10, 5), 6.0, BoundaryCondition.Interzone, B);

    private static readonly HorizontalSurface GroundFloor =
        new(new SurfaceId("A/F2"), A, HorizontalKind.Floor, Polygon2.Rectangle(0, 0, 10, 5), 0.0, BoundaryCondition.Ground, null);

    private static readonly HorizontalSurface AdiabaticCeiling =
        new(new SurfaceId("A/C2"), A, HorizontalKind.Ceiling, Polygon2.Rectangle(0, 0, 10, 5), 3.0, BoundaryCondition.Adiabatic, null);

    [Fact]
    public void BothOptionsAreOffByDefault()
    {
        Assert.False(HeatTransferOptions.Default.EnableInternalWallHeatTransfer);
        Assert.False(HeatTransferOptions.Default.EnableFloorHeatTransfer);
        Assert.Equal(HeatTransferOptions.Default, new HeatTransferOptions());
    }

    [Theory]
    [InlineData(false, false, "Adiabatic", "Adiabatic", "Adiabatic")]
    [InlineData(true, false, "Interzone:B", "Adiabatic", "Adiabatic")]
    [InlineData(false, true, "Adiabatic", "Interzone:B", "Interzone:B")]
    [InlineData(true, true, "Interzone:B", "Interzone:B", "Interzone:B")]
    public void InterzoneSurfacesAreAdiabaticUnlessTheirOptionIsOn(bool walls, bool floors, string wall, string floor, string ceiling)
    {
        var options = new HeatTransferOptions(walls, floors);

        Assert.Equal(wall, Text(options.Exported(InterzoneWall)));
        Assert.Equal(floor, Text(options.Exported(InterzoneFloor)));
        Assert.Equal(ceiling, Text(options.Exported(InterzoneCeiling)));
        Assert.Equal("Outdoors", Text(options.Exported(OutdoorWall)));
        Assert.Equal("Ground", Text(options.Exported(GroundFloor)));
        Assert.Equal("Adiabatic", Text(options.Exported(AdiabaticCeiling)));
    }

    [Fact]
    public void TheSurfaceIsNotChanged()
    {
        new HeatTransferOptions().Exported(InterzoneWall);

        Assert.Equal(BoundaryCondition.Interzone, InterzoneWall.Boundary);
        Assert.Equal(B, InterzoneWall.AdjacentZone);
    }

    [Fact]
    public void DescribeNamesBothOptions()
    {
        Assert.Equal("EnableInternalWallHeatTransfer=True EnableFloorHeatTransfer=False", new HeatTransferOptions(true, false).Describe());
    }

    private static string Text(ExportedBoundary boundary) =>
        boundary.AdjacentZone is { } adjacent ? $"{boundary.Boundary}:{adjacent.Value}" : boundary.Boundary.ToString();
}
```

```csharp
using Lod.Core.Common;
using Lod.Core.Model;

namespace Lod.Core.Conversion;

/// <summary>The boundary a converter writes for a surface.</summary>
/// <param name="Boundary">Boundary condition.</param>
/// <param name="AdjacentZone">Zone on the other side when <paramref name="Boundary"/> is <see cref="BoundaryCondition.Interzone"/>, otherwise <c>null</c>.</param>
public readonly record struct ExportedBoundary(BoundaryCondition Boundary, ZoneId? AdjacentZone);

/// <summary>
/// Heat transfer between zones as a conversion option (D-069), shared by every converter (<c>Convert2BEM</c>, <c>Convert2IDF</c>). The building keeps
/// its interzone surfaces; the options only decide what a converter writes, and the converter records the values it used.
/// </summary>
/// <param name="EnableInternalWallHeatTransfer">Write walls between two zones paired with the adjacent zone; otherwise adiabatic.</param>
/// <param name="EnableFloorHeatTransfer">Write floors and ceilings between two zones paired with the adjacent zone; otherwise adiabatic.</param>
public sealed record HeatTransferOptions(bool EnableInternalWallHeatTransfer = false, bool EnableFloorHeatTransfer = false)
{
    /// <summary>Both options off (D-069).</summary>
    public static HeatTransferOptions Default { get; } = new();

    /// <summary>
    /// The boundary to write for a surface: an interzone wall is adiabatic unless <see cref="EnableInternalWallHeatTransfer"/>, an interzone floor or
    /// ceiling is adiabatic unless <see cref="EnableFloorHeatTransfer"/>, and every other surface keeps its boundary.
    /// </summary>
    /// <param name="surface">A surface of a building.</param>
    /// <returns>The exported boundary.</returns>
    public ExportedBoundary Exported(Surface surface)
    {
        bool keep = surface.Boundary != BoundaryCondition.Interzone
            || (surface is WallSurface ? EnableInternalWallHeatTransfer : EnableFloorHeatTransfer);
        return keep ? new ExportedBoundary(surface.Boundary, surface.AdjacentZone) : new ExportedBoundary(BoundaryCondition.Adiabatic, null);
    }

    /// <summary>The options as one line for a converter's provenance record.</summary>
    /// <returns>The text.</returns>
    public string Describe() =>
        $"{nameof(EnableInternalWallHeatTransfer)}={EnableInternalWallHeatTransfer} {nameof(EnableFloorHeatTransfer)}={EnableFloorHeatTransfer}";
}
```

```csharp
using System;
using Lod.Core.Buildings;
using Lod.Core.Common;

namespace Lod.Grasshopper.Components;

public sealed class StackedFloorZoneMultiplierComponent : FloorAggregatorComponent
{
    public StackedFloorZoneMultiplierComponent()
        : base(
            "Stacked Floor Zone Multiplier",
            "ZMult",
            "Representative storeys with zone multipliers (D-071, formerly Floor Area Multiplier). Each floor type stands for N storeys; floors and ceilings are designated on the whole stack (D-068), storeys with exposed floors or ceilings (ground, roof, setbacks, overhangs) are split off as x1 storeys so exposure is never multiplied (D-046), and storeys keep their true elevations (D-045). Pieces between storeys are adiabatic.")
    {
    }

    public override Guid ComponentGuid => new("e4b44821-c9ed-49b9-9c4e-7c08355254dd");

    protected override string MultiplierDescription => "How many storeys each floor type stands for. Default 1.";

    protected override IFloorAggregator CreateAggregator() => new StackedFloorZoneMultiplier(ToleranceSettings.Default);
}

public sealed class SingleZonePerFloorTypeComponent : FloorAggregatorComponent
{
    public SingleZonePerFloorTypeComponent()
        : base(
            "Single Zone per Floor Type",
            "1ZType",
            "One zone per floor entry (D-071): the storeys of entry i merge into zone E{i}. Slabs within an entry become internal mass (D-031), walls between merged zones are discarded (D-034), and floors and ceilings between entries stay between two zones (D-068).")
    {
    }

    public override Guid ComponentGuid => new("506d5037-de93-4aee-a6c4-47dcc97509e1");

    protected override string MultiplierDescription => "How many storeys each floor stands for (stacked explicitly before merging). Default 1.";

    protected override IFloorAggregator CreateAggregator() => new SingleZonePerFloorType(ToleranceSettings.Default);
}

public sealed class SingleZoneBuildingComponent : FloorAggregatorComponent
{
    public SingleZoneBuildingComponent()
        : base(
            "Single Zone Building",
            "1ZBldg",
            "The whole building as one zone, BUILDING (D-021, D-071): slabs become internal mass (D-031), walls between merged zones are discarded (D-034).")
    {
    }

    public override Guid ComponentGuid => new("50965154-e31e-4730-b61c-15a6f40bee6f");

    protected override string MultiplierDescription => "How many storeys each floor stands for (stacked explicitly before merging). Default 1.";

    protected override IFloorAggregator CreateAggregator() => new SingleZoneBuilding(ToleranceSettings.Default);
}
```

- [x] **Step 1: Create `src/Lod.Grasshopper/Convert/PlanMotionDecomposition.cs` (full file)**

```csharp
using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using Lod.Core.Common;
using Rhino.Geometry;

namespace Lod.Grasshopper.Convert;

/// <summary>The parts of a Rhino transform that <c>PlanTransform</c> applies, and what else the transform contains.</summary>
/// <param name="RotationDegrees">Rotation about the world z axis, degrees, counter-clockwise seen from above.</param>
/// <param name="TranslationX">Translation along world x, m.</param>
/// <param name="TranslationY">Translation along world y, m.</param>
/// <param name="Ignored">The parts of the transform that are not applied, empty for a rotation about z followed by an x-y translation.</param>
internal sealed record PlanMotionParts(double RotationDegrees, double TranslationX, double TranslationY, IReadOnlyList<string> Ignored);

/// <summary>
/// Splits a Rhino transform into the rotation about the world z axis and the translation in the world x-y plane (D-070). The rotation is
/// <c>atan2(M10, M00)</c>; the translation is (<c>M03</c>, <c>M13</c>) converted from model units to metres with the same unit scale the preview
/// uses. Whatever else the transform holds is listed, never applied: a translation along z (more than <see cref="ToleranceSettings.Distance"/>),
/// a plan part that is not a pure rotation (scaling, shear, or mirroring; entries compared within <see cref="ToleranceSettings.Angle"/>), a rotation
/// about another axis or scaling along z, and a projection.
/// </summary>
internal static class PlanMotionDecomposition
{
    /// <summary>Decomposes a transform given in model units.</summary>
    /// <param name="transform">The transform.</param>
    /// <param name="unitScale">Metres to model units, <see cref="RhinoGeometry.UnitScale"/>.</param>
    /// <param name="tolerances">Tolerances.</param>
    /// <returns>The applied parts and the ignored ones.</returns>
    public static PlanMotionParts Decompose(Transform transform, double unitScale, ToleranceSettings tolerances)
    {
        double angle = Math.Atan2(transform.M10, transform.M00);
        double cos = Math.Cos(angle);
        double sin = Math.Sin(angle);
        bool Near(double actual, double expected) => Math.Abs(actual - expected) <= tolerances.Angle;
        var ignored = new List<string>();
        if (Math.Abs(transform.M23 / unitScale) > tolerances.Distance)
        {
            ignored.Add("a translation along z");
        }

        if (!Near(transform.M00, cos) || !Near(transform.M11, cos) || !Near(transform.M10, sin) || !Near(transform.M01, -sin))
        {
            ignored.Add("scaling, shear, or mirroring in plan");
        }

        if (!Near(transform.M02, 0.0) || !Near(transform.M12, 0.0) || !Near(transform.M20, 0.0) || !Near(transform.M21, 0.0) || !Near(transform.M22, 1.0))
        {
            ignored.Add("a rotation about another axis or scaling along z");
        }

        if (!Near(transform.M30, 0.0) || !Near(transform.M31, 0.0) || !Near(transform.M32, 0.0) || !Near(transform.M33, 1.0))
        {
            ignored.Add("a projection");
        }

        return new PlanMotionParts(angle * 180.0 / Math.PI, transform.M03 / unitScale, transform.M13 / unitScale, ignored);
    }
}
```
