```csharp
using System;
using System.Drawing;
using System.Globalization;
using Grasshopper.Kernel;
using Lod.Core.Common;
using Lod.Core.Model;
using Lod.Core.Plans;
using Lod.Grasshopper.Convert;
using Lod.Grasshopper.Goo;
using Lod.Grasshopper.Parameters;
using Rhino.Geometry;

namespace Lod.Grasshopper.Components;

/// <summary>
/// Moves and rotates a plan (D-070): applies the rotation about the world z axis and the x-y translation of a Grasshopper transform through
/// <see cref="PlanTransform"/>, and warns about anything else the transform contains, which is not applied.
/// </summary>
public sealed class TransformPlanComponent : GH_Component
{
    public TransformPlanComponent()
        : base(
            "Transform Plan",
            "TPlan",
            "Rotates a plan about the world z axis and moves it in the world x-y plane (D-070). Any other part of the transform (z translation, rotation about another axis, scaling, shear, mirroring, projection) is not applied and gives a warning. Storey elevations come from the floor aggregator.",
            ComponentCategories.Category,
            ComponentCategories.Generate)
    {
    }

    public override Guid ComponentGuid => new("57c17b9c-04b4-4595-9150-bb923877d95e");

    protected override Bitmap? Icon => IconLibrary.For(this);

    protected override void RegisterInputParams(GH_InputParamManager pManager)
    {
        pManager.AddParameter(new PlanParameter(), "Plan", "Plan", "The plan to move.", GH_ParamAccess.item);
        pManager.AddTransformParameter("Transform", "T", "Rotation about the world z axis and translation in x and y, in model units; other parts are not applied.", GH_ParamAccess.item);
        pManager.AddParameter(ComponentSupport.PreviewLocationParameter());
    }

    protected override void RegisterOutputParams(GH_OutputParamManager pManager)
    {
        pManager.AddParameter(new PlanParameter(), "Plan", "Plan", "The moved plan.", GH_ParamAccess.item);
    }

    protected override void SolveInstance(IGH_DataAccess DA)
    {
        PlanGoo? plan = null;
        Transform transform = Transform.Identity;
        if (!DA.GetData(0, ref plan) || plan is null || !DA.GetData(1, ref transform))
        {
            return;
        }

        PlanMotionParts parts = PlanMotionDecomposition.Decompose(transform, RhinoGeometry.UnitScale, ToleranceSettings.Default);
        if (parts.Ignored.Count > 0)
        {
            AddRuntimeMessage(
                GH_RuntimeMessageLevel.Warning,
                string.Format(
                    CultureInfo.InvariantCulture,
                    "Not applied (D-070): {0}. Applied: a rotation of {1:0.######}° about the world z axis and a translation of ({2:0.######}, {3:0.######}) m in x and y.",
                    string.Join(", ", parts.Ignored),
                    parts.RotationDegrees,
                    parts.TranslationX,
                    parts.TranslationY));
        }

        IGeneratedPlan? moved = ComponentSupport.Unwrap(this, PlanTransform.Apply(plan.Value, parts.RotationDegrees, parts.TranslationX, parts.TranslationY));
        if (moved is not null)
        {
            DA.SetData(0, new PlanGoo(moved, ComponentSupport.PreviewLocation(this, DA)));
        }
    }
}

```csharp
using System;
using System.Drawing;
using System.Linq;
using Grasshopper;
using Grasshopper.Kernel;
using Grasshopper.Kernel.Data;
using Lod.Core.Common;
using Lod.Core.Conversion;
using Lod.Core.Loads;
using Lod.Core.Model;
using Lod.Core.Validation;
using Lod.Grasshopper.Convert;
using Lod.Grasshopper.Goo;
using Lod.Grasshopper.Parameters;
using Rhino.Geometry;
using Surface = Lod.Core.Model.Surface;

namespace Lod.Grasshopper.Components;

/// <summary>
/// Converts a building into neutral Grasshopper data for a ClimateStudio definition (D-023, provisional): one tree branch per zone,
/// in building zone order. The output layout is documented in docs/architecture/convert2bem.md. The building is validated first; a failed
/// validation blocks conversion unless Override is set, and the override is recorded in the Provenance output (GLOBAL.md, scientific rule 6).
/// Interzone surfaces are written as adiabatic unless the heat-transfer options say otherwise (D-069, <see cref="HeatTransferOptions"/>); the
/// building itself is not changed, and the options are recorded in the Provenance output.
/// </summary>
public sealed class Convert2BemComponent : GH_Component
{
    public Convert2BemComponent()
        : base(
            "Convert2BEM",
            "2BEM",
            "Building to zone geometry, programs, and surfaces as data trees (branch {i} = zone i), ready to wire into a ClimateStudio definition. Provisional layout (D-023).",
            ComponentCategories.Category,
            ComponentCategories.Convert)
    {
    }

    public override Guid ComponentGuid => new("d091d70c-4de3-4f29-a900-ea4c9b777ba3");

    protected override Bitmap? Icon => IconLibrary.For(this);

    protected override void RegisterInputParams(GH_InputParamManager pManager)
    {
        pManager.AddParameter(new BuildingParameter(), "Building", "Bldg", "The building.", GH_ParamAccess.item);
        pManager.AddBooleanParameter("Override", "Ovr", "Convert even if validation fails; the override is recorded.", GH_ParamAccess.item, false);
        pManager.AddBooleanParameter(
            nameof(HeatTransferOptions.EnableInternalWallHeatTransfer),
            "IWHT",
            "Write walls between two zones paired with the adjacent zone (Interzone:<zone ID>); when false they are written as Adiabatic (D-069).",
            GH_ParamAccess.item,
            false);
        pManager.AddBooleanParameter(
            nameof(HeatTransferOptions.EnableFloorHeatTransfer),
            "FHT",
            "Write floors and ceilings between two zones paired with the adjacent zone (Interzone:<zone ID>); when false they are written as Adiabatic (D-069).",
            GH_ParamAccess.item,
            false);
    }

    protected override void RegisterOutputParams(GH_OutputParamManager pManager)
    {
        pManager.AddBrepParameter("Zones", "Z", "{i}: closed Brep of each part of zone i, model units.", GH_ParamAccess.tree);
        pManager.AddTextParameter("Names", "N", "{i}: zone ID.", GH_ParamAccess.tree);
        pManager.AddTextParameter("Space Types", "ST", "{i}: space type.", GH_ParamAccess.tree);
        pManager.AddIntegerParameter("Multipliers", "M", "{i}: zone multiplier.", GH_ParamAccess.tree);
        pManager.AddBooleanParameter("Conditioned", "Cd", "{i}: whether zone i is conditioned (D-038).", GH_ParamAccess.tree);
        pManager.AddTextParameter("Load Types", "LT", "{i}: load types, aligned with Load Values.", GH_ParamAccess.tree);
        pManager.AddTextParameter("Load Bases", "LB", "{i}: load bases.", GH_ParamAccess.tree);
        pManager.AddNumberParameter("Load Values", "LV", "{i}: design values in their basis.", GH_ParamAccess.tree);
        pManager.AddNumberParameter("Load Schedules", "LS", "{i;k}: 8760 fractions of load k of zone i.", GH_ParamAccess.tree);
        pManager.AddNumberParameter("Heating Setpoints", "HS", "{i}: 8760 heating setpoints, °C; empty for unconditioned zones.", GH_ParamAccess.tree);
        pManager.AddNumberParameter("Cooling Setpoints", "CS", "{i}: 8760 cooling setpoints, °C; empty for unconditioned zones.", GH_ParamAccess.tree);
        pManager.AddBrepParameter("Surfaces", "S", "{i}: walls, floors, and ceilings of zone i.", GH_ParamAccess.tree);
        pManager.AddTextParameter("Boundaries", "B", "{i}: boundary written for each surface: Outdoors, Ground, Adiabatic, or Interzone:<zone ID>; interzone surfaces are Adiabatic unless their heat-transfer option is on (D-069).", GH_ParamAccess.tree);
        pManager.AddBrepParameter("Windows", "W", "{i}: explicit windows of zone i, at their positions.", GH_ParamAccess.tree);
        pManager.AddNumberParameter("Internal Mass", "IM", "{i}: exposed internal-mass area of zone i (slab area × exposed faces), m².", GH_ParamAccess.tree);
        pManager.AddTextParameter("Provenance", "P", "Building provenance, validation report, heat-transfer options, and any override.", GH_ParamAccess.item);
    }

    protected override void SolveInstance(IGH_DataAccess DA)
    {
        BuildingGoo? goo = null;
        bool overrideValidation = false;
        bool internalWalls = false;
        bool floors = false;
        if (!DA.GetData(0, ref goo) || goo is null || !DA.GetData(1, ref overrideValidation) || !DA.GetData(2, ref internalWalls) || !DA.GetData(3, ref floors))
        {
            return;
        }

        IGeneratedBuilding building = goo.Value;
        var options = new HeatTransferOptions(internalWalls, floors);
        ValidationReport report = new BuildingValidator(ToleranceSettings.Default).Validate(building);
        string record = building.Provenance.Describe() + report.Describe() + "OPTIONS: " + options.Describe() + "\n";
        if (!report.Passed && !overrideValidation)
        {
            ComponentSupport.Report(this, new[] { Diagnostic.Error(DiagnosticCodes.ValidationFailed, "Validation failed; conversion blocked. See Provenance, or set Override.") });
            DA.SetData(15, record);
            return;
        }

        if (!report.Passed)
        {
            ComponentSupport.Report(this, new[] { Diagnostic.Warning(DiagnosticCodes.ValidationOverridden, "Validation failed; converted because Override is set. The override is recorded in Provenance.") });
            record += "OVERRIDE: converted despite failed validation\n";
        }

        var zones = new DataTree<Brep>();
        var names = new DataTree<string>();
        var spaceTypes = new DataTree<string>();
        var multipliers = new DataTree<int>();
        var conditioned = new DataTree<bool>();
        var loadTypes = new DataTree<string>();
        var loadBases = new DataTree<string>();
        var loadValues = new DataTree<double>();
        var loadSchedules = new DataTree<double>();
        var heating = new DataTree<double>();
        var cooling = new DataTree<double>();
        var surfaces = new DataTree<Brep>();
        var boundaries = new DataTree<string>();
        var windows = new DataTree<Brep>();
        var mass = new DataTree<double>();

        for (int i = 0; i < building.Zones.Count; i++)
        {
            Zone zone = building.Zones[i];
            var path = new GH_Path(i);
            zones.AddRange(RhinoGeometry.ZoneBreps(zone), path);
            names.Add(zone.Id.Value, path);
            spaceTypes.Add(zone.SpaceType.ToString(), path);
            multipliers.Add(zone.Multiplier, path);
            conditioned.Add(zone.Program.IsConditioned, path);
            for (int k = 0; k < zone.Program.Loads.Count; k++)
            {
                LoadDefinition load = zone.Program.Loads[k];
                loadTypes.Add(load.Type.ToString(), path);
                loadBases.Add(load.Basis.ToString(), path);
                loadValues.Add(load.Value, path);
                loadSchedules.AddRange(load.Schedule.Values, new GH_Path(i, k));
            }

            heating.EnsurePath(path);
            cooling.EnsurePath(path);
            if (zone.Program.Thermostat is { } thermostat)
            {
                heating.AddRange(thermostat.HeatingSetpoint.Values, path);
                cooling.AddRange(thermostat.CoolingSetpoint.Values, path);
            }
            foreach (Surface surface in building.Surfaces.Where(s => s.Zone == zone.Id))
            {
                foreach (Brep brep in SurfaceBreps(surface))
                {
                    surfaces.Add(brep, path);
                    ExportedBoundary boundary = options.Exported(surface);
                    boundaries.Add(boundary.AdjacentZone is { } adjacent ? $"{boundary.Boundary}:{adjacent.Value}" : boundary.Boundary.ToString(), path);
                }

                if (surface is WallSurface wall)
                {
                    windows.AddRange(RhinoGeometry.WindowBreps(wall), path);
                }
            }

            mass.Add(building.InternalMasses.Where(m => m.Zone == zone.Id).Sum(m => m.SlabArea * m.ExposedFaces), path);
        }

        DA.SetDataTree(0, zones);
        DA.SetDataTree(1, names);
        DA.SetDataTree(2, spaceTypes);
        DA.SetDataTree(3, multipliers);
        DA.SetDataTree(4, conditioned);
        DA.SetDataTree(5, loadTypes);
        DA.SetDataTree(6, loadBases);
        DA.SetDataTree(7, loadValues);
        DA.SetDataTree(8, loadSchedules);
        DA.SetDataTree(9, heating);
        DA.SetDataTree(10, cooling);
        DA.SetDataTree(11, surfaces);
        DA.SetDataTree(12, boundaries);
        DA.SetDataTree(13, windows);
        DA.SetDataTree(14, mass);
        DA.SetData(15, record);
    }

    private static Brep[] SurfaceBreps(Surface surface) => surface switch
    {
        WallSurface wall => RhinoGeometry.WallBrep(wall) is { } brep ? new[] { brep } : new Brep[0],
        HorizontalSurface horizontal => RhinoGeometry.HorizontalBreps(horizontal).ToArray(),
        _ => new Brep[0],
    };
}
```

**Files:**

- Modify: `src/Lod.Grasshopper/Components/AggregatorComponents.cs` (Task 12), `src/Lod.Grasshopper/Components/PipelineComponents.cs` (S2 file, last changed in S4.1), `src/Lod.Grasshopper/Convert/RhinoGeometry.cs` (S2 file, last changed in S4.1), `src/Lod.Grasshopper/Goo/BemGoo.cs` (S2 file, last changed in S4.1)

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `StackedFloorZoneMultiplier.UnmodelledStoreys` (Task 6); `ZonePart` (`Footprint`, `Elevation`, `Height`), `Polygon2.Rings`, `Point2.DistanceTo` (S2); `ToleranceSettings.Default.Distance`; `RhinoGeometry.PlanarBreps`, `UnitScale` (S2); `PreviewGoo<T>` with `PreviewOffset` (S4.1); RhinoCommon `Brep.CreateBooleanUnion`, `Brep.MergeCoplanarFaces`.
- Produces:
  - `protected virtual IReadOnlyList<ZonePart> FloorAggregatorComponent.UnmodelledParts(IReadOnlyList<FloorEntry> entries)` (none by default), passed into the building's Goo; *Stacked Floor Zone Multiplier* overrides it with the parts of `UnmodelledStoreys`.
  - `BuildingGoo(IGeneratedBuilding value, Vector3d previewOffset = default, IReadOnlyList<ZonePart>? unmodelled = null)`, kept by `Duplicate`; `PreviewGoo<T>` draws the unmodelled parts in grey `150, 150, 150` with transparency 0.85, and a zone with several parts as merged volumes. Data, *Inspect*, *Validate*, and *Convert2BEM* are unchanged.
  - `RhinoGeometry.PartBreps(IEnumerable<ZonePart>)` (one closed Brep per part; `ZoneBreps` uses it) and `RhinoGeometry.MergedZoneBreps(Zone)`: the zone's vertical runs extruded once each; a boolean union of the runs, with coplanar faces merged, only when there are several runs, falling back to the runs when the union fails.
  - `internal static class VerticalRuns` with `Of(IEnumerable<ZonePart> parts, ToleranceSettings tolerances) : IReadOnlyList<ZonePart>`: consecutive parts, bottom to top, whose outlines are the same polygon (vertices within `Distance`, rings in the same cyclic order) and that start at the top of the part below merge into one part over their total height. Preview only.

```csharp
using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.Drawing;
using System.Linq;
using Grasshopper.Kernel;
using Grasshopper.Kernel.Types;
using Lod.Core.Buildings;
using Lod.Core.Common;
using Lod.Core.Model;
using Lod.Core.Programs;
using Lod.Core.Simplification;
using Lod.Generators.Linear;
using Lod.Grasshopper.Goo;
using Lod.Grasshopper.Parameters;

namespace Lod.Grasshopper.Components;

public sealed class LinearPlanGeneratorComponent : GH_Component
{
    public LinearPlanGeneratorComponent()
        : base(
            "Linear Plan Generator",
            "Linear",
            "Detailed linear block plan: west stair, double-loaded corridor, one zone per dwelling unit. All lengths in metres regardless of model units.",
            ComponentCategories.Category,
            ComponentCategories.Generate)
    {
    }

    public override Guid ComponentGuid => new("a4a9131e-0a0f-49e0-99a4-f26760f6810f");

    protected override Bitmap? Icon => IconLibrary.For(this);

    protected override void RegisterInputParams(GH_InputParamManager pManager)
    {
        pManager.AddNumberParameter("Length", "L", "Plan length along X, m.", GH_ParamAccess.item, 24.0);
        pManager.AddNumberParameter("Unit Depth", "UD", "Depth of each unit row, m.", GH_ParamAccess.item, 8.0);
        pManager.AddNumberParameter("Corridor Width", "CW", "Corridor width, m.", GH_ParamAccess.item, 2.0);
        pManager.AddNumberParameter("Unit Width", "UW", "Target unit width, m; the row is divided evenly.", GH_ParamAccess.item, 10.0);
        pManager.AddNumberParameter("Stair Length", "SL", "Length of the west stair, m.", GH_ParamAccess.item, 4.0);
        pManager.AddNumberParameter("Floor Height", "FH", "Floor-to-floor height, m.", GH_ParamAccess.item, 3.0);
        pManager.AddNumberParameter("Orientation", "O", "Clockwise rotation of plan north from true north, degrees.", GH_ParamAccess.item, 0.0);
        pManager.AddParameter(new ProgramPresetParameter(), "Dwelling Unit", "DU", "Program preset of the dwelling units; space type DwellingUnit.", GH_ParamAccess.item);
        pManager.AddParameter(new ProgramPresetParameter(), "Corridor", "Co", "Program preset of the corridor; space type Corridor.", GH_ParamAccess.item);
        pManager.AddParameter(new ProgramPresetParameter(), "Stair", "St", "Program preset of the stair; space type Stair.", GH_ParamAccess.item);
        pManager.AddParameter(ComponentSupport.PreviewLocationParameter());
    }

    protected override void RegisterOutputParams(GH_OutputParamManager pManager)
    {
        pManager.AddParameter(new PlanParameter(), "Plan", "Plan", "The detailed plan.", GH_ParamAccess.item);
    }

    protected override void SolveInstance(IGH_DataAccess DA)
    {
        double[] values = new double[7];
        for (int i = 0; i < values.Length; i++)
        {
            if (!DA.GetData(i, ref values[i]))
            {
                return;
            }
        }

        ProgramPresetGoo? dwellingUnit = null;
        ProgramPresetGoo? corridor = null;
        ProgramPresetGoo? stair = null;
        if (!DA.GetData(7, ref dwellingUnit) || !DA.GetData(8, ref corridor) || !DA.GetData(9, ref stair)
            || dwellingUnit is null || corridor is null || stair is null)
        {
            return;
        }

        var parameters = new LinearPlanParameters(values[0], values[1], values[2], values[3], values[4], values[5], values[6]);
        var presets = new LinearPlanPresets(dwellingUnit.Value, corridor.Value, stair.Value);
        IGeneratedPlan? plan = ComponentSupport.Unwrap(this, new LinearPlanGenerator(ToleranceSettings.Default).Generate(parameters, presets));
        if (plan is not null)
        {
            DA.SetData(0, new PlanGoo(plan, ComponentSupport.PreviewLocation(this, DA)));
        }
    }
}

public sealed class NoSimplificationComponent : GH_Component
{

        : base("No Simplification", "Z0", "Keeps the detailed plan's zones and surfaces (Z0).", ComponentCategories.Category, ComponentCategories.Simplify)
    {
    }

    public override Guid ComponentGuid => new("83270abe-a13f-4f4c-a893-79739ad6e006");

    protected override Bitmap? Icon => IconLibrary.For(this);

    protected override void RegisterInputParams(GH_InputParamManager pManager)
    {
        pManager.AddParameter(new PlanParameter(), "Plan", "Plan", "Detailed plan.", GH_ParamAccess.item);
        pManager.AddParameter(ComponentSupport.PreviewLocationParameter());
    }

    protected override void RegisterOutputParams(GH_OutputParamManager pManager)
    {
        pManager.AddParameter(new FloorParameter(), "Floor", "Floor", "The floor.", GH_ParamAccess.item);
    }

    protected override void SolveInstance(IGH_DataAccess DA)
    {
        PlanGoo? plan = null;
        if (!DA.GetData(0, ref plan) || plan is null)
        {
            return;
        }

        IFloor? floor = ComponentSupport.Unwrap(this, new NoSimplification().Simplify(plan.Value));
        if (floor is not null)
        {
            DA.SetData(0, new FloorGoo(floor, ComponentSupport.PreviewLocation(this, DA)));
        }
    }
}

/// <summary>Shared inputs and solving of the floor aggregator components: floors bottom to top and their multipliers.</summary>
public abstract class FloorAggregatorComponent : GH_Component
{
    protected FloorAggregatorComponent(string name, string nickname, string description)
        : base(name, nickname, description, ComponentCategories.Category, ComponentCategories.Aggregate)
    {
    }

    protected override Bitmap? Icon => IconLibrary.For(this);

    protected abstract string MultiplierDescription { get; }

    protected override void RegisterInputParams(GH_InputParamManager pManager)
    {
        pManager.AddParameter(new FloorParameter(), "Floors", "F", "Floors, bottom to top.", GH_ParamAccess.list);
        pManager.AddIntegerParameter("Multipliers", "N", MultiplierDescription, GH_ParamAccess.list);
        pManager[1].Optional = true;
        pManager.AddParameter(ComponentSupport.PreviewLocationParameter());
    }

    protected override void RegisterOutputParams(GH_OutputParamManager pManager)
    {
        pManager.AddParameter(new BuildingParameter(), "Building", "Bldg", "The building.", GH_ParamAccess.item);
    }

    protected override void SolveInstance(IGH_DataAccess DA)
    {
        var floors = new List<FloorGoo>();
        var multipliers = new List<int>();
        if (!DA.GetDataList(0, floors) || floors.Any(f => f is null))
        {
            return;
        }

        DA.GetDataList(1, multipliers);
        if (multipliers.Count != 0 && multipliers.Count != floors.Count)
        {
            AddRuntimeMessage(GH_RuntimeMessageLevel.Error, $"Give one multiplier per floor ({floors.Count}), or none for all 1.");
            return;
        }

        FloorEntry[] entries = floors.Select((f, i) => new FloorEntry(f.Value, multipliers.Count == 0 ? 1 : multipliers[i])).ToArray();
        IGeneratedBuilding? building = ComponentSupport.Unwrap(this, CreateAggregator().Aggregate(entries));
        if (building is not null)
        {
            DA.SetData(0, new BuildingGoo(building, ComponentSupport.PreviewLocation(this, DA), UnmodelledParts(entries)));
        }
    }

    protected abstract IFloorAggregator CreateAggregator();

    /// <summary>Parts of the storeys the building represents but does not model, for its preview; none unless the method leaves storeys out.</summary>
    protected virtual IReadOnlyList<ZonePart> UnmodelledParts(IReadOnlyList<FloorEntry> entries) => new ZonePart[0];
}

public sealed class StackFloorsComponent : FloorAggregatorComponent
{
    public StackFloorsComponent()
        : base("Stack Floors", "Stack", "All storeys explicit: each floor is repeated by its multiplier and stacked; inter-storey surfaces are interzone.")
    {
    }

    public override Guid ComponentGuid => new("6ce6524d-09b0-49fc-9b88-4c15bdcc8797");

    protected override string MultiplierDescription => "How many storeys each floor stands for (repeated explicitly). Default 1.";

    protected override IFloorAggregator CreateAggregator() => new Stack(ToleranceSettings.Default);
}

public sealed class InspectComponent : GH_Component
{
    public InspectComponent()
        : base("Inspect", "Inspect", "Text report and provenance of a plan, floor, or building.", ComponentCategories.Category, ComponentCategories.Inspect)
    {
    }

    public override Guid ComponentGuid => new("1d56a553-7a66-451f-90e2-81e744b43bdd");

    protected override Bitmap? Icon => IconLibrary.For(this);

    protected override void RegisterInputParams(GH_InputParamManager pManager)
    {
        pManager.AddGenericParameter("Object", "O", "A plan, floor, or building.", GH_ParamAccess.item);
    }

    protected override void RegisterOutputParams(GH_OutputParamManager pManager)
    {
        pManager.AddTextParameter("Report", "R", "Zones, loads, and surfaces.", GH_ParamAccess.item);
        pManager.AddTextParameter("Provenance", "P", "How the object was produced, with code version.", GH_ParamAccess.item);
    }

    protected override void SolveInstance(IGH_DataAccess DA)
    {
        IGH_Goo? goo = null;
        if (!DA.GetData(0, ref goo) || goo is null)
        {
            return;
        }

        (string Report, Provenance Provenance)? described = goo switch
        {
            PlanGoo plan => (TextReport.Describe(plan.Value), plan.Value.Provenance),
            FloorGoo floor => (TextReport.Describe(floor.Value), floor.Value.Provenance),
            BuildingGoo building => (TextReport.Describe(building.Value), building.Value.Provenance),
            _ => null,
        };
        if (described is null)
        {
            AddRuntimeMessage(GH_RuntimeMessageLevel.Error, $"Expected a plan, floor, or building, got {goo.TypeName}.");
            return;
        }

        DA.SetData(0, described.Value.Report);
        DA.SetData(1, described.Value.Provenance.Describe());
    }
}

Each floor type stands for N storeys; floors and ceilings are designated on the whole stack (D-068), storeys with exposed floors or ceilings (ground, roof, setbacks, overhangs) are split off as x1 storeys so exposure is never multiplied (D-046), and storeys keep their true elevations (D-045). - [x] **Step 3: Group stacked parts — create `src/Lod.Grasshopper/Convert/VerticalRuns.cs` (full file)**

```csharp
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.Linq;
using Lod.Core.Common;
using Lod.Core.Geometry;
using Lod.Core.Model;

namespace Lod.Grasshopper.Convert;

/// <summary>
/// Preview-only grouping of a zone's parts into vertical runs (D-071): consecutive parts, bottom to top, whose outlines are the same polygon
/// (every vertex within <see cref="ToleranceSettings.Distance"/>, rings in the same cyclic order) and where each part starts at the top of the one
/// below (within <see cref="ToleranceSettings.Distance"/>) become one part spanning their total height. The zone's data is not changed.
/// </summary>
internal static class VerticalRuns
{
    /// <summary>The parts merged into maximal vertical runs of one outline, in the order the runs start.</summary>
    /// <param name="parts">A zone's parts.</param>
    /// <param name="tolerances">Tolerances.</param>
    /// <returns>One part per run.</returns>
    public static IReadOnlyList<ZonePart> Of(IEnumerable<ZonePart> parts, ToleranceSettings tolerances)
    {
        var runs = new List<ZonePart>();
        foreach (ZonePart part in parts.OrderBy(p => p.Elevation))
        {
            int index = runs.FindIndex(r =>
                System.Math.Abs(r.Elevation + r.Height - part.Elevation) <= tolerances.Distance && SameOutline(r.Footprint, part.Footprint, tolerances.Distance));
            if (index >= 0)
            {
                runs[index] = runs[index] with { Height = runs[index].Height + part.Height };
            }
            else
            {
                runs.Add(part);
            }
        }

        return runs;
    }

    private static bool SameOutline(Polygon2 a, Polygon2 b, double distance)
    {
        IReadOnlyList<Point2>[] ringsA = a.Rings.ToArray();
        IReadOnlyList<Point2>[] ringsB = b.Rings.ToArray();
        return ringsA.Length == ringsB.Length && ringsA.Zip(ringsB, (x, y) => SameRing(x, y, distance)).All(same => same);
    }

    private static bool SameRing(IReadOnlyList<Point2> a, IReadOnlyList<Point2> b, double distance)
    {
        if (a.Count != b.Count)
        {
            return false;
        }

        for (int shift = 0; shift < b.Count; shift++)
        {
            if (Enumerable.Range(0, a.Count).All(i => a[i].DistanceTo(b[(i + shift) % b.Count]) <= distance))
            {
                return true;
            }
        }

        return false;
    }
}
```

- [x] **Step 4: Merged volumes — replace `src/Lod.Grasshopper/Convert/RhinoGeometry.cs` (full file)**

```csharp
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.Linq;
using Lod.Core.Common;
using Lod.Core.Geometry;
using Lod.Core.Model;
using Rhino;
using Rhino.Geometry;

namespace Lod.Grasshopper.Convert;

/// <summary>
/// Converts pipeline geometry (metres) to Rhino geometry in the active document's model units. The only place Rhino geometry is created.
/// </summary>
internal static class RhinoGeometry
{
    private static double Tolerance => ToleranceSettings.Default.Distance;

    /// <summary>Metres to model units of the active document (1 without a document).</summary>
    public static double UnitScale => RhinoDoc.ActiveDoc is { } doc ? RhinoMath.UnitScale(UnitSystem.Meters, doc.ModelUnitSystem) : 1.0;

    /// <summary>One closed Brep per zone part.</summary>
    public static IEnumerable<Brep> ZoneBreps(Zone zone) => PartBreps(zone.Parts);

    /// <summary>
    /// A zone as merged volumes, for the viewport preview of zones spanning storeys (D-071): parts stacked with the same outline form one vertical
    /// run (<see cref="VerticalRuns"/>), extruded once over the run's height, so no boolean union of touching solids is needed. Only where outlines
    /// differ between runs is a boolean union of the runs tried, with coplanar faces merged; if it fails, the runs are shown. Data outputs keep one
    /// Brep per part.
    /// </summary>
    public static IEnumerable<Brep> MergedZoneBreps(Zone zone)
    {
        Brep[] runs = PartBreps(VerticalRuns.Of(zone.Parts, ToleranceSettings.Default)).ToArray();
        if (runs.Length < 2)
        {
            return runs;
        }

        double tolerance = Tolerance * UnitScale;
        Brep[]? union = Brep.CreateBooleanUnion(runs, tolerance);
        if (union is null || union.Length == 0)
        {
            return runs;
        }

        foreach (Brep brep in union)
        {
            brep.MergeCoplanarFaces(tolerance);
        }

        return union;
    }

    /// <summary>One closed Brep per part: its floor polygon extruded by its height.</summary>
    public static IEnumerable<Brep> PartBreps(IEnumerable<ZonePart> parts)
    {
        foreach (ZonePart part in parts)
        {
            foreach (Brep planar in PlanarBreps(part.Footprint, part.Elevation))
            {
                Brep? solid = planar.Faces[0].CreateExtrusion(new LineCurve(Point3d.Origin, new Point3d(0, 0, part.Height)), true);
                if (solid is not null)
                {
                    yield return Scaled(solid);
                }
            }
        }
    }

    /// <summary>The wall as a planar rectangle.</summary>
    public static Brep? WallBrep(WallSurface wall) =>
        Rectangle(wall, offset: 0.0, width: wall.Length, bottom: wall.Elevation, height: wall.Height);

    /// <summary>Every window of a wall at its explicit position.</summary>
    public static IEnumerable<Brep> WindowBreps(WallSurface wall) =>
        wall.Windows
            .Select(w => Rectangle(wall, offset: w.Offset, width: w.Width, bottom: wall.Elevation + w.SillHeight, height: w.Height))
            .Where(b => b is not null)
            .Select(b => b!);

    /// <summary>A floor or ceiling as planar Breps.</summary>
    public static IEnumerable<Brep> HorizontalBreps(HorizontalSurface surface) => PlanarBreps(surface.Polygon, surface.Elevation).Select(Scaled);

    private static IEnumerable<Brep> PlanarBreps(Polygon2 polygon, double elevation)
    {
        Curve[] curves = polygon.Rings
            .Select(ring => (Curve)new PolylineCurve(ring.Select(p => new Point3d(p.X, p.Y, elevation)).Concat(new[] { new Point3d(ring[0].X, ring[0].Y, elevation) })))
            .ToArray();
        return Brep.CreatePlanarBreps(curves, Tolerance) ?? Enumerable.Empty<Brep>();
    }

    private static Brep? Rectangle(WallSurface wall, double offset, double width, double bottom, double height)
    {
        double length = wall.Length;
        var direction = new Vector3d((wall.End.X - wall.Start.X) / length, (wall.End.Y - wall.Start.Y) / length, 0);
        var origin = new Point3d(wall.Start.X, wall.Start.Y, bottom) + (direction * offset);
        Point3d a = origin;
        Point3d b = origin + (direction * width);
        Point3d c = b + new Vector3d(0, 0, height);
        Point3d d = a + new Vector3d(0, 0, height);
        Brep? brep = Brep.CreateFromCornerPoints(a, b, c, d, Tolerance);
        return brep is null ? null : Scaled(brep);
    }

    private static Brep Scaled(Brep brep)
    {
        double scale = UnitScale;
        if (scale != 1.0)
        {
            brep.Transform(Transform.Scale(Point3d.Origin, scale));
        }

        return brep;
    }
}
```

```csharp
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.Drawing;
using System.Linq;
using Grasshopper.Kernel;
using Grasshopper.Kernel.Types;
using Lod.Core.Loads;
using Lod.Core.Model;
using Lod.Core.Programs;
using Lod.Core.Schedules;
using Lod.Grasshopper.Convert;
using Rhino.Display;
using Rhino.Geometry;
using Surface = Lod.Core.Model.Surface;

namespace Lod.Grasshopper.Goo;

/// <summary>Grasshopper wrapper of an immutable pipeline object. Values are never modified, so duplicates share the value.</summary>
/// <typeparam name="T">Wrapped type.</typeparam>
public abstract class BemGoo<T> : GH_Goo<T>
    where T : class
{
    protected BemGoo(T value)
        : base(value)
    {
    }

    public override bool IsValid => Value is not null;

    public override string TypeDescription => $"BEMGen {TypeName}";
}

public sealed class ScheduleGoo : BemGoo<Schedule>
{
    public ScheduleGoo(Schedule value)
        : base(value)
    {
    }

    public override string TypeName => "Schedule";

    public override IGH_Goo Duplicate() => new ScheduleGoo(Value);

    public override string ToString() => $"Schedule {Value.Name} ({Value.Kind})";
}

public sealed class LoadGoo : BemGoo<LoadDefinition>
{
    public LoadGoo(LoadDefinition value)
        : base(value)
    {
    }

    public override string TypeName => "Load";

    public override IGH_Goo Duplicate() => new LoadGoo(Value);

    public override string ToString() => $"Load {Value.Type} {Value.Value} {Value.Basis}";
}

public sealed class ProgramPresetGoo : BemGoo<ProgramPreset>
{
    public ProgramPresetGoo(ProgramPreset value)
        : base(value)
    {
    }

    public override string TypeName => "Program Preset";

    public override IGH_Goo Duplicate() => new ProgramPresetGoo(Value);

    public override string ToString() => $"Preset {Value.Name} ({Value.SpaceType}, WWR {Value.WindowToWallRatio})";
}

/// <summary>
/// Wrapper with viewport preview: zones coloured by space type (a zone with several parts as one merged volume, D-071), storeys that are represented
/// but not modelled in transparent grey (D-071, D-075), windows in blue. The preview is moved by <see cref="PreviewOffset"/> (D-062); the wrapped
/// value keeps its true coordinates.
/// </summary>
/// <typeparam name="T">Wrapped type.</typeparam>
public abstract class PreviewGoo<T> : BemGoo<T>, IGH_PreviewData
    where T : class
{
    private static readonly Color WindowColour = Color.FromArgb(70, 140, 220);
    private static readonly Color UnmodelledColour = Color.FromArgb(150, 150, 150);
    private List<(Brep Brep, DisplayMaterial Material)>? _preview;

    protected PreviewGoo(T value, Vector3d previewOffset)
        : base(value)
    {
        PreviewOffset = previewOffset;
    }

    /// <summary>Translation of the viewport preview only, in model units (D-062).</summary>
    public Vector3d PreviewOffset { get; }

    public BoundingBox ClippingBox
    {
        get
        {
            BoundingBox box = BoundingBox.Empty;
            foreach ((Brep brep, DisplayMaterial _) in Preview)
            {
                box.Union(brep.GetBoundingBox(false));
            }

            return box;
        }
    }

    private List<(Brep Brep, DisplayMaterial Material)> Preview => _preview ??= BuildPreview();

    public void DrawViewportMeshes(GH_PreviewMeshArgs args)
    {
        foreach ((Brep brep, DisplayMaterial material) in Preview)
        {
            args.Pipeline.DrawBrepShaded(brep, material);
        }
    }

    public void DrawViewportWires(GH_PreviewWireArgs args)
    {
        foreach ((Brep brep, DisplayMaterial _) in Preview)
        {
            args.Pipeline.DrawBrepWires(brep, args.Color);
        }
    }

    protected abstract IEnumerable<Zone> Zones { get; }

    protected abstract IEnumerable<Surface> Surfaces { get; }

    /// <summary>Parts of storeys that are represented but not modelled, drawn transparent grey; none unless a building says so.</summary>
    protected virtual IEnumerable<ZonePart> UnmodelledParts => Enumerable.Empty<ZonePart>();

    private List<(Brep, DisplayMaterial)> BuildPreview()
    {
        var items = new List<(Brep, DisplayMaterial)>();
        foreach (Zone zone in Zones)
        {
            var material = new DisplayMaterial(SpaceTypeColour(zone.SpaceType), 0.6);
            IEnumerable<Brep> volumes = zone.Parts.Count > 1 ? RhinoGeometry.MergedZoneBreps(zone) : RhinoGeometry.ZoneBreps(zone);
            items.AddRange(volumes.Select(b => (b, material)));
        }

        var unmodelled = new DisplayMaterial(UnmodelledColour, 0.85);
        items.AddRange(RhinoGeometry.PartBreps(UnmodelledParts).Select(b => (b, unmodelled)));

        var glass = new DisplayMaterial(WindowColour, 0.2);
        foreach (WallSurface wall in Surfaces.OfType<WallSurface>())
        {
            items.AddRange(RhinoGeometry.WindowBreps(wall).Select(b => (b, glass)));
        }

        if (!PreviewOffset.IsZero)
        {
            Transform move = Transform.Translation(PreviewOffset);
            foreach ((Brep brep, DisplayMaterial _) in items)
            {
                brep.Transform(move);
            }
        }

        return items;
    }

    private static Color SpaceTypeColour(SpaceType type) => type switch
    {
        SpaceType.DwellingUnit => Color.FromArgb(240, 200, 120),
        SpaceType.Corridor => Color.FromArgb(180, 180, 180),
        SpaceType.Stair => Color.FromArgb(200, 120, 120),
        SpaceType.Core => Color.FromArgb(160, 120, 200),
        SpaceType.Mixed => Color.FromArgb(120, 190, 160),
        _ => Color.FromArgb(150, 170, 200),
    };
}

public sealed class PlanGoo : PreviewGoo<IGeneratedPlan>
{
    public PlanGoo(IGeneratedPlan value, Vector3d previewOffset = default)
        : base(value, previewOffset)
    {
    }

    public override string TypeName => "Plan";

    protected override IEnumerable<Zone> Zones => Value.Zones;

    protected override IEnumerable<Surface> Surfaces => Value.Surfaces;

    public override IGH_Goo Duplicate() => new PlanGoo(Value, PreviewOffset);

    public override string ToString() => $"Plan ({Value.Zones.Count} zones, {Value.Provenance.Operation})";
}

public sealed class FloorGoo : PreviewGoo<IFloor>
{
    public FloorGoo(IFloor value, Vector3d previewOffset = default)
        : base(value, previewOffset)
    {
    }

    public override string TypeName => "Floor";

    protected override IEnumerable<Zone> Zones => Value.Zones;

    protected override IEnumerable<Surface> Surfaces => Value.Surfaces;

    public override IGH_Goo Duplicate() => new FloorGoo(Value, PreviewOffset);

    public override string ToString() => $"Floor ({Value.Zones.Count} zones, {Value.Provenance.Operation})";
}

public sealed class BuildingGoo : PreviewGoo<IGeneratedBuilding>
{
    private readonly IReadOnlyList<ZonePart> _unmodelled;

    /// <param name="value">The building.</param>
    /// <param name="previewOffset">Preview Location (D-062).</param>
    /// <param name="unmodelled">Parts of the storeys the building represents but does not model, from the aggregator (preview only).</param>
    public BuildingGoo(IGeneratedBuilding value, Vector3d previewOffset = default, IReadOnlyList<ZonePart>? unmodelled = null)
        : base(value, previewOffset)
    {
        _unmodelled = unmodelled ?? new ZonePart[0];
    }

    public override string TypeName => "Building";

    protected override IEnumerable<Zone> Zones => Value.Zones;

    protected override IEnumerable<Surface> Surfaces => Value.Surfaces;

    protected override IEnumerable<ZonePart> UnmodelledParts => _unmodelled;

    public override IGH_Goo Duplicate() => new BuildingGoo(Value, PreviewOffset, _unmodelled);

    public override string ToString() => $"Building ({Value.Zones.Count} zones, {Value.Provenance.Operation})";
}

- [x] **Step 2: Replace `scripts/make_icons.py` (full file)**

```python
"""Draws the BEMGen Grasshopper icons (D-063).

Every icon is drawn at 96 x 96 pixels on a transparent background and downsampled to 24 x 24 with LANCZOS, then written to
src/Lod.Grasshopper/Icons/<Name>.png, where <Name> is the class name of the component, parameter, or assembly info that shows it.
Each icon is a dark glyph plus the accent colour of its toolbar panel; a parameter uses the panel of the component that makes its object.
Shapes only, no fonts, so the output does not depend on installed fonts.

Run from the repository root:  python scripts/make_icons.py
Requires Python 3.10+ and Pillow. Generated with Pillow 12.3.0; the same Pillow version gives the same pixels.
The script stops with an error when an icon has no class in src/Lod.Grasshopper or a class has no icon.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import re
import sys
from collections.abc import Callable
from pathlib import Path

from PIL import Image, ImageDraw

ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parent.parent
SOURCE = ROOT / "src" / "Lod.Grasshopper"
OUTPUT = SOURCE / "Icons"

CANVAS = 96
SIZE = 24
LINE = 8  # 2 px after downsampling

DARK = (38, 38, 38, 255)
WHITE = (255, 255, 255, 255)

# One accent per toolbar panel (ComponentCategories).
INFO = (96, 125, 139, 255)
PROGRAM = (230, 140, 30, 255)
GENERATE = (46, 158, 79, 255)
SIMPLIFY = (47, 123, 216, 255)
AGGREGATE = (138, 79, 200, 255)
CONVERT = (208, 69, 58, 255)
INSPECT = (24, 150, 150, 255)

Colour = tuple[int, int, int, int]
Draw = ImageDraw.ImageDraw
Motif = Callable[[Draw, Colour], None]
Painter = Callable[[Image.Image, Colour], None]


def box(d: Draw, xy: tuple[float, float, float, float], fill=None, outline=DARK, width: int = LINE) -> None:
    d.rectangle(xy, fill=fill, outline=outline, width=width)


def line(d: Draw, points: list[tuple[float, float]], width: int = LINE, fill=DARK) -> None:
    d.line(points, fill=fill, width=width, joint="curve")


def arrow_head(d: Draw, tip: tuple[float, float], direction: str, size: float, fill=DARK) -> None:
    x, y = tip
    if direction == "right":
        d.polygon([(x, y), (x - size, y - size), (x - size, y + size)], fill=fill)
    elif direction == "down":
        d.polygon([(x, y), (x - size, y - size), (x + size, y - size)], fill=fill)


# --- motifs: each draws inside the 96 x 96 canvas -------------------------------------------------------------


def info(d: Draw, accent) -> None:
    d.ellipse((6, 6, 90, 90), fill=accent, outline=DARK, width=LINE)
    d.rectangle((42, 40, 54, 74), fill=WHITE)
    d.rectangle((42, 20, 54, 32), fill=WHITE)


def assembly(d: Draw, accent) -> None:
    # A stepped block: the building at three levels of detail.
    box(d, (8, 52, 88, 88), fill=accent)
    box(d, (8, 30, 60, 56), fill=accent)
    box(d, (8, 8, 36, 34), fill=accent)


def schedule(d: Draw, accent) -> None:
    # A 24-hour profile as bars on a time axis.
    heights = [16, 48, 72, 36, 60, 76, 24]
    for i, h in enumerate(heights):
        x = 12 + i * 12
        d.rectangle((x, 84 - h, x + 7, 83), fill=accent)
    d.rectangle((4, 84, 92, 91), fill=DARK)
    d.rectangle((4, 4, 11, 91), fill=DARK)


def load(d: Draw, accent) -> None:
    bolt = [(58, 4), (18, 54), (44, 54), (34, 92), (78, 38), (52, 38), (64, 4)]
    d.polygon(bolt, fill=accent, outline=DARK, width=6)


def preset_tag(d: Draw, accent) -> None:
    tag = [(8, 30), (30, 8), (88, 8), (88, 88), (30, 88), (8, 66)]
    d.polygon(tag, fill=accent, outline=DARK, width=LINE)
    d.ellipse((24, 40, 40, 56), fill=WHITE, outline=DARK, width=4)
    line(d, [(52, 34), (76, 34)], width=6)
    line(d, [(52, 48), (76, 48)], width=6)
    line(d, [(52, 62), (76, 62)], width=6)


def house(d: Draw, accent) -> None:
    d.polygon([(48, 6), (90, 44), (6, 44)], fill=accent, outline=DARK, width=LINE)
    box(d, (16, 42, 80, 90), fill=accent)
    d.rectangle((40, 60, 56, 90), fill=DARK)


def corridor(d: Draw, accent) -> None:
    box(d, (4, 30, 92, 66), fill=accent)
    line(d, [(16, 48), (60, 48)], width=8)
    arrow_head(d, (78, 48), "right", 12)


def stair(d: Draw, accent) -> None:
    steps = [(6, 90), (6, 70), (28, 70), (28, 50), (50, 50), (50, 30), (72, 30), (72, 10), (90, 10), (90, 90)]
    d.polygon(steps, fill=accent, outline=DARK, width=LINE)


def linear_generator(d: Draw, accent) -> None:
    # Footprint with a west stair, a corridor band, and dwelling-unit divisions on both sides.
    box(d, (4, 12, 91, 83))
    d.rectangle((12, 20, 23, 75), fill=DARK)
    d.rectangle((28, 40, 87, 55), fill=accent)
    for x in (48, 68):
        line(d, [(x, 12), (x, 40)])
        line(d, [(x, 55), (x, 83)])


def transform_plan(d: Draw, accent) -> None:
    # A footprint outline and the same footprint rotated and moved, joined by a turning arrow.
    box(d, (6, 52, 50, 90), width=6)
    d.polygon([(66, 12), (92, 28), (76, 56), (50, 40)], fill=accent, outline=DARK, width=6)
    d.arc((18, 20, 70, 72), start=180, end=270, fill=DARK, width=8)
    arrow_head(d, (58, 24), "right", 10)


def z0(d: Draw, accent) -> None:
    # Many cells: every unit its own zone.
    box(d, (4, 4, 91, 91), fill=accent)
    for k in (32, 60):
        line(d, [(k, 4), (k, 91)])
        line(d, [(4, k), (91, k)])


def z1(d: Draw, accent) -> None:
    # Cells of one space type merged: two unit rows and a corridor.
    box(d, (4, 10, 92, 40), fill=accent)
    box(d, (4, 56, 92, 86), fill=accent)
    box(d, (4, 36, 92, 60))


def z2(d: Draw, accent) -> None:
    # Perimeter ring split on the corner bisectors, plus a core.
    box(d, (4, 4, 92, 92))
    d.rectangle((30, 30, 66, 66), fill=accent, outline=DARK, width=LINE)
    for a, b in [((4, 4), (30, 30)), ((92, 4), (66, 30)), ((4, 92), (30, 66)), ((92, 92), (66, 66))]:
        line(d, [a, b], width=6)


def z3(d: Draw, accent) -> None:
    box(d, (6, 16, 90, 80), fill=accent)


def slab(d: Draw, accent, y: float, height: float = 16, left: float = 6, right: float = 90) -> None:
    box(d, (left, y, right, y + height), fill=accent, width=6)


def stack(d: Draw, accent) -> None:
    for y in (8, 36, 64):
        slab(d, accent, y, 24)


def multiplier(d: Draw, accent) -> None:
    slab(d, accent, 6, 24, 6, 90)
    line(d, [(18, 44), (50, 84)], width=10)
    line(d, [(50, 44), (18, 84)], width=10)
    for y in (44, 58, 72):
        d.rectangle((62, y, 90, y + 8), fill=DARK)


def single_zone_per_floor_type(d: Draw, accent) -> None:
    # One zone per floor entry: a one-storey zone below a three-storey zone.
    box(d, (24, 66, 71, 91), fill=accent)
    box(d, (24, 4, 71, 58), fill=accent)


def single_zone_building(d: Draw, accent) -> None:
    box(d, (24, 4, 71, 91), fill=accent)


def convert(d: Draw, accent) -> None:
    d.polygon([(40, 44), (90, 44), (90, 92), (40, 92)], fill=accent, outline=DARK, width=LINE)
    line(d, [(6, 14), (44, 14), (64, 34), (64, 56)], width=10)
    arrow_head(d, (64, 74), "down", 16)


def inspect(d: Draw, accent) -> None:
    d.ellipse((6, 6, 64, 64), fill=accent, outline=DARK, width=LINE)
    d.ellipse((18, 18, 38, 38), fill=WHITE)
    line(d, [(56, 56), (88, 88)], width=16)


def mini_grid(d: Draw, origin: tuple[float, float], size: float, cells: int, accent) -> None:
    x0, y0 = origin
    box(d, (x0, y0, x0 + size, y0 + size), fill=accent, width=6)
    step = size / cells
    for k in range(1, cells):
        line(d, [(x0 + k * step, y0), (x0 + k * step, y0 + size)], width=4)
        line(d, [(x0, y0 + k * step), (x0 + size, y0 + k * step)], width=4)


def map_source_target(d: Draw, accent) -> None:
    mini_grid(d, (4, 4), 40, 2, accent)
    box(d, (52, 52, 92, 92), fill=accent, width=6)
    line(d, [(32, 56), (32, 72), (40, 72)], width=8)
    arrow_head(d, (52, 72), "right", 12)
    line(d, [(56, 26), (72, 26), (72, 36)], width=8)
    arrow_head(d, (72, 50), "down", 12)


def validate(d: Draw, accent) -> None:
    d.ellipse((4, 4, 92, 92), fill=accent, outline=DARK, width=LINE)
    line(d, [(24, 50), (42, 68), (74, 30)], width=14, fill=WHITE)


# Parameter motifs: small versions of their object's motif, drawn inside the hexagon (x and y in 20..76).


def small_schedule(d: Draw, accent) -> None:
    for x, h in ((28, 16), (40, 36), (52, 24), (64, 40)):
        d.rectangle((x, 72 - h, x + 7, 71), fill=accent)
    d.rectangle((24, 72, 75, 75), fill=DARK)


def small_load(d: Draw, accent) -> None:
    d.polygon([(54, 20), (30, 52), (46, 52), (40, 76), (68, 44), (52, 44), (60, 20)], fill=accent, outline=DARK, width=4)


def small_preset(d: Draw, accent) -> None:
    d.polygon([(20, 36), (32, 24), (76, 24), (76, 72), (32, 72), (20, 60)], fill=accent, outline=DARK, width=4)
    d.ellipse((30, 42, 42, 54), fill=WHITE, outline=DARK, width=4)


def small_plan(d: Draw, accent) -> None:
    box(d, (20, 24, 75, 71), width=4)
    d.rectangle((24, 44, 71, 51), fill=accent)
    d.rectangle((24, 28, 31, 67), fill=DARK)
    for x in (48,):
        line(d, [(x, 24), (x, 44)], width=4)
        line(d, [(x, 52), (x, 71)], width=4)


def small_floor(d: Draw, accent) -> None:
    box(d, (16, 36, 79, 59), fill=accent, width=4)


def small_building(d: Draw, accent) -> None:
    box(d, (32, 16, 63, 79), fill=accent, width=4)
    for y in (36, 56):
        line(d, [(32, y), (63, y)], width=4)


# --- icons ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------


def component(motif: Motif) -> Painter:
    """A component: its motif at full size."""

    def paint(image: Image.Image, accent: Colour) -> None:
        motif(ImageDraw.Draw(image), accent)

    return paint


def parameter(motif: Motif) -> Painter:
    """A parameter: a small version of its object's motif inside a hexagon (the Grasshopper parameter shape)."""

    def paint(image: Image.Image, accent: Colour) -> None:
        d = ImageDraw.Draw(image)
        d.polygon([(24, 4), (72, 4), (94, 48), (72, 92), (24, 92), (2, 48)], fill=WHITE, outline=DARK, width=4)
        motif(d, accent)

    return paint


ICONS: dict[str, tuple[Colour, Painter]] = {
    "BemGenAssemblyInfo": (INFO, component(assembly)),
    "BemGenInfoComponent": (INFO, component(info)),
    "ScheduleComponent": (PROGRAM, component(schedule)),
    "LoadComponent": (PROGRAM, component(load)),
    "ProgramPresetComponent": (PROGRAM, component(preset_tag)),
    "DwellingUnitPresetComponent": (PROGRAM, component(house)),
    "CorridorPresetComponent": (PROGRAM, component(corridor)),
    "StairPresetComponent": (PROGRAM, component(stair)),
    "LinearPlanGeneratorComponent": (GENERATE, component(linear_generator)),
    "TransformPlanComponent": (GENERATE, component(transform_plan)),
    "NoSimplificationComponent": (SIMPLIFY, component(z0)),
    "SemanticMergeComponent": (SIMPLIFY, component(z1)),
    "PerimeterCoreComponent": (SIMPLIFY, component(z2)),
    "SingleZonePerFloorComponent": (SIMPLIFY, component(z3)),
    "StackFloorsComponent": (AGGREGATE, component(stack)),
    "StackedFloorZoneMultiplierComponent": (AGGREGATE, component(multiplier)),
    "SingleZonePerFloorTypeComponent": (AGGREGATE, component(single_zone_per_floor_type)),
    "SingleZoneBuildingComponent": (AGGREGATE, component(single_zone_building)),
    "Convert2BemComponent": (CONVERT, component(convert)),
    "InspectComponent": (INSPECT, component(inspect)),
    "MapSourceToTargetComponent": (INSPECT, component(map_source_target)),
    "ValidateComponent": (INSPECT, component(validate)),
    "ScheduleParameter": (PROGRAM, parameter(small_schedule)),
    "LoadParameter": (PROGRAM, parameter(small_load)),
    "ProgramPresetParameter": (PROGRAM, parameter(small_preset)),
    "PlanParameter": (GENERATE, parameter(small_plan)),
    "FloorParameter": (SIMPLIFY, parameter(small_floor)),
    "BuildingParameter": (AGGREGATE, parameter(small_building)),
}


def render(name: str) -> Image.Image:
    accent, paint = ICONS[name]
    image = Image.new("RGBA", (CANVAS, CANVAS), (0, 0, 0, 0))
    paint(image, accent)
    return image.resize((SIZE, SIZE), Image.Resampling.LANCZOS)


def classes_with_icons() -> set[str]:
    """Concrete classes that show an icon: components, parameters, and the assembly info."""
    pattern = re.compile(r"public sealed class (\w+) : (\w+)")
    names = set()
    for path in SOURCE.rglob("*.cs"):
        if "obj" in path.parts or "bin" in path.parts:
            continue
        for name, _ in pattern.findall(path.read_text(encoding="utf-8")):
            if name.endswith(("Component", "Parameter")) or name == "BemGenAssemblyInfo":
                names.add(name)
    return names


def main() -> int:
    expected = classes_with_icons()
    missing = sorted(expected - ICONS.keys())
    unknown = sorted(ICONS.keys() - expected)
    if missing or unknown:
        print(f"Classes without an icon: {missing}; icons without a class: {unknown}", file=sys.stderr)
        return 1

    OUTPUT.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    stale = sorted(p.name for p in OUTPUT.glob("*.png") if p.stem not in ICONS)
    if stale:
        print(f"Icons without a class in {OUTPUT}: {stale}", file=sys.stderr)
        return 1

    for name in sorted(ICONS):
        render(name).save(OUTPUT / f"{name}.png", optimize=False)
    print(f"Wrote {len(ICONS)} icons to {OUTPUT.relative_to(ROOT)}")
    return 0


if __name__ == "__main__":
    sys.exit(main())
```

- [x] **Step 6: Look at the icons**

Draw a contact sheet of all icons at 8× (nearest neighbour) into the build folder, which git ignores:

```bash
python - <<'EOF'
from pathlib import Path
from PIL import Image
icons = sorted(Path("src/Lod.Grasshopper/Icons").glob("*.png"))
cell = 24 * 8 + 16
sheet = Image.new("RGBA", (6 * cell, -(-len(icons) // 6) * cell), (235, 235, 235, 255))
for i, path in enumerate(icons):
    big = Image.open(path).convert("RGBA").resize((24 * 8, 24 * 8), Image.Resampling.NEAREST)
    sheet.alpha_composite(big, ((i % 6) * cell + 8, (i // 6) * cell + 8))
Path("src/Lod.Grasshopper/bin").mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
sheet.save("src/Lod.Grasshopper/bin/icon-sheet.png")
print(len(icons), "icons drawn on src/Lod.Grasshopper/bin/icon-sheet.png")
EOF
```

Expected: `28 icons drawn on src/Lod.Grasshopper/bin/icon-sheet.png`. Open the image. In the order of `sorted()` on Windows (case-insensitive), six per row, the 26 icons of S4.1 look as in the S4.1 plan (Task 7, Step 5), two of them under new names, and the two new ones are added; row 4 and row 5 read: row 4 holds the Schedule parameter, *Semantic Merge*, *Single Zone Building* (one tall purple block, the former *Single Zone Merged* icon), *Single Zone per Floor* (one blue block), *Single Zone per Floor Type* (a tall purple block above a short one), and *Stacked Floor Zone Multiplier* (purple slab, "×", three bars, the former *Floor Area Multiplier* icon); row 5 holds *Stack Floors* (three purple slabs), *Stair Preset*, *Transform Plan* (a dark footprint outline at the lower left, a dark arc, and a green turned square at the upper right), and *Validate*. *Single Zone per Floor Type* and *Single Zone Building* are told apart at 24 × 24, and *Transform Plan* has the green of *Linear Plan Generator*.

If the hashes of Step 5 differed (another Pillow version), this check decides: when every icon matches the description above, continue and record the Pillow version in the commit message body; otherwise stop and report the difference. The script is never edited to match hashes.

   with

```markdown
- Walls carry explicit windows (position along the wall, sill, width, height; any number per wall, D-039). This generator's rule: one centered window per outdoor wall with area = the zone preset's WWR × wall area (D-026).
- Transform (D-070): `PlanTransform.Apply` rotates a plan about the world origin by an angle about +z (counter-clockwise seen from above, degrees) and then moves it in x and y (metres). Footprint, zones, walls, and floors and ceilings move rigidly (`PlanMotion`; whole quarter turns are exact); windows keep their offsets, sills, and sizes; IDs are kept. Wall facing follows the rotated geometry; the plan's *Orientation* (true north) is unchanged. Provenance: operation `TransformPlan`, parameters `RotationDegrees`, `TranslationX`, `TranslationY`, the source plan as input. A value that is not finite is the error `InvalidParameter`. Only plans are transformed; floors and buildings keep the geometry of their plans, and storey elevations come from the floor aggregator.
```

   with

```markdown
Every aggregator first assembles the full stack (`Storeys.Assemble`, D-068): each entry repeated by its multiplier, storey `k` at the sum of the floor-to-floor heights below it, zone and surface IDs prefixed `L{k}/`. Every floor and ceiling piece is designated there: split by overlap with the zones of the storeys below and above into pieces between two storeys (`Interzone`), uncovered parts `Outdoors`, the floors of storey 0 `Ground`, and the ceilings of the top storey roofs (`Outdoors`). A storey `k > 0` whose floor area covered by storey `k − 1` differs from its floor area by more than the relative area tolerance gets the warning `UnsupportedStorey`, naming the storey and the uncovered area in m² (D-074); the building is still produced. The four methods differ in what they model:

| Aggregator | Storeys and zones | Multiplier Nᵢ | Floors and ceilings | Walls and windows | Internal mass |
```

The component applies the rotation about the world z axis, `atan2(M10, M00)`, and the x-y translation (`M03`, `M13`) converted to metres with the preview's unit scale (`PlanMotionDecomposition`). A translation along z, a plan part that is not a pure rotation (scaling, shear, mirroring), a rotation about another axis or scaling along z, and a projection are not applied; they are named in one warning, `Not applied (D-070): …. Applied: a rotation of …° about the world z axis and a translation of (…, …) m in x and y.` Matrix entries are compared within `ToleranceSettings.Angle`, the z translation within `Distance`.
```

Written for every surface between two zones whose heat-transfer option is off (the default, D-069), and for the floors and ceilings of *Stacked Floor Zone Multiplier* storeys that lie between two storeys, which are adiabatic in the building itself whatever the options say (D-075). |
| `Interzone:<zone ID>` | Another zone of the same building, e.g. `Interzone:L1/US1`: walls between zones when *EnableInternalWallHeatTransfer* is on, floors and ceilings between storeys (or, for *Single Zone per Floor Type*, between floor entries) when *EnableFloorHeatTransfer* is on. Every interzone surface has a matching surface in the other zone that refers back to it. |
```

9. Replace

```markdown
There is no separate roof condition: a roof is an `Outdoors` ceiling. `Unresolved` marks floors and ceilings of plans and floors before stacking; a building from a floor aggregator has none.
```

   with

```markdown
There is no separate roof condition: a roof is an `Outdoors` ceiling. `Unresolved` marks floors and ceilings of plans and floors before stacking; a building from a floor aggregator has none.

## Heat transfer between zones

Since S4.2 (D-069) heat transfer between zones is a choice of the conversion, not of the building model. The building keeps its interzone surfaces, and its validation does not depend on the options. *Convert2BEM* writes each surface's boundary through `HeatTransferOptions.Exported` in `Lod.Core` (namespace `Lod.Core.Conversion`), which *Convert2IDF* (S6) uses too:

| Surface | IWHT `false`, FHT `false` (default) | IWHT `true` | FHT `true` |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| Wall between two zones | `Adiabatic` | `Interzone:<zone ID>` | `Adiabatic` |
| Floor or ceiling between two zones | `Adiabatic` | `Adiabatic` | `Interzone:<zone ID>` |
| Any other surface | its boundary | its boundary | its boundary |

For the canonical linear plan with Multipliers 1, 3, 1 and *Stack Floors*, the 90 interior walls and the 48 floors and ceilings between storeys are `Adiabatic` by default; for *Single Zone per Floor Type* the 24 floor and ceiling pieces between the three entries are; *Single Zone Building* has no interzone surface, so the options change nothing.
```

10. Replace

```markdown
An `InternalMass` object (D-031) describes a slab whose faces both lie inside one zone. This happens only when `SingleZoneMerged` (S4, D-021) merges all storeys into one zone: the slabs between storeys are then no longer boundaries between two zones and become internal mass of the single zone, keeping their construction and area (D-031; ADR-009 in S4). Each object records the slab area (one face) and the number of faces exposed to the zone air. *Internal Mass* gives, per zone, the sum of slab area × exposed faces: the slab surface area that exchanges heat with the zone air, in m². Walls between merged zones are discarded, not turned into internal mass (D-034).
```

   with

```markdown
An `InternalMass` object (D-031) describes a slab whose faces both lie inside one zone. This happens when a single-zone aggregator merges storeys into one zone: *Single Zone Building* (all storeys, D-021) and *Single Zone per Floor Type* (the storeys of one floor entry, D-071). The slabs between storeys of that zone are then no longer boundaries between two zones and become internal mass, keeping their construction and area (D-031; ADR-009, ADR-013). Each object records the slab area (one face) and the number of faces exposed to the zone air. *Internal Mass* gives, per zone, the sum of slab area × exposed faces: the slab surface area that exchanges heat with the zone air, in m². Walls between merged zones are discarded, not turned into internal mass (D-034).
```

11. Replace

```markdown
*Stack Floors* creates no internal mass, so in S2 the output is 0 for every zone; `SingleZoneMerged` populates it from S4.
```

   with

```markdown
*Stack Floors* and *Stacked Floor Zone Multiplier* create no internal mass, so their output is 0 for every zone.
```

Passed: the conversion runs and `Provenance` (output 15) holds the provenance tree followed by the report. Failed with `Override` false (the default): error `ValidationFailed`, no conversion (outputs 0–14 stay empty), and only `Provenance` is set so the failing checks can be read. Failed with `Override` true: warning `ValidationOverridden`, the conversion runs, and `Provenance` ends with the line `OVERRIDE: converted despite failed validation`. The override is recorded only in that text, so keep `Provenance` with any result produced from an overridden model.

The override is recorded only in that text, so keep `Provenance` with any result produced from an overridden model.

```markdown
### S4.2 checklist — aggregation methods, plan transform, and heat-transfer options (`v0.4.2`)

Build the plugin with `dotnet build src/Lod.Grasshopper -c Release` (0 warnings), close Rhino, and start it again with the plugin loaded as described above. Use a Rhino model in metres. The canonical floor is: *Dwelling Unit Preset*, *Corridor Preset*, and *Stair Preset* → *Linear Plan Generator* with its default inputs (24 m × 18 m, 3 m storeys) → *No Simplification*. To give an aggregator the same floor three times, pass it through *Duplicate Data* (Sets › List) with Number = 3 and give Multipliers as a panel with the lines `1`, `3`, `1`. Definitions saved before S4.2 keep their components (D-064): a *Floor Area Multiplier* opens as *Stacked Floor Zone Multiplier*, and a *Single Zone Merged* opens as *Single Zone per Floor Type*, which now makes one zone per floor entry instead of one zone for the building.

#### Toolbar and icons

- [ ] *2 Generate* shows *Linear Plan Generator* and *Transform Plan* (TPlan). *4 Aggregate* shows *Stack Floors* (Stack), *Stacked Floor Zone Multiplier* (ZMult), *Single Zone per Floor Type* (1ZType), and *Single Zone Building* (1ZBldg); *Floor Area Multiplier* and *Single Zone Merged* are gone.
- [ ] *Transform Plan* has a green icon (a footprint, a turning arrow, and the turned footprint); *Single Zone per Floor Type* (a short block below a tall one) and *Single Zone Building* (one tall block) have purple icons that are told apart at toolbar size.

#### Aggregation methods and previews

1. Connect the canonical floor three times with Multipliers 1, 8, 1 to *Stacked Floor Zone Multiplier*.
   - [ ] No runtime message. *Inspect* lists zones `L0/…`, `L5/…` (`multiplier=8`), and `L9/…`; its Provenance output starts with `StackedFloorZoneMultiplier Multipliers=1,8,1 Storeys=L0x1,L5x8,L9x1`. *Validate* gives Passed = True.
   - [ ] The preview shows the whole 30 m building (bounding box 0,0,0 to 24,18,30): the modelled storeys at 0, 15, and 27 m in their space-type colours with their windows, and the storeys at 3 to 15 m and 18 to 27 m in transparent grey, without windows.
   - [ ] With *Preview Location* `30,0,0` the coloured and the grey storeys move 30 m east together; *Convert2BEM* still outputs zone Breps between 0,0,0 and 24,18,30.
2. Connect the canonical floor three times with Multipliers 1, 3, 1 to *Single Zone Building*.
   - [ ] No runtime message. The preview shows one closed volume in the Mixed colour from 0 to 15 m (bounding box 0,0,0 to 24,18,15) without horizontal seams at the slab levels, with the plan's windows on every storey.
   - [ ] *Inspect* starts its zone list with `zone BUILDING Mixed "Building" area=2160.000000 volume=6480.000000 parts=5 multiplier=1` and lists four lines `mass BUILDING area=432.000000 faces=2` at z = 3, 6, 9, and 12; its Provenance output starts with `SingleZoneBuilding Multipliers=1,3,1`. *Convert2BEM* outputs five Breps in *Zones* {0} and the *Internal Mass* value 3456.
3. Connect the same floors and multipliers to *Single Zone per Floor Type*.
   - [ ] No runtime message. *Inspect* lists three zones: `zone E0 Mixed "Floor type 0" area=432.000000 volume=1296.000000 parts=1`, `zone E1 Mixed "Floor type 1" area=1296.000000 volume=3888.000000 parts=3`, and `zone E2 Mixed "Floor type 2" area=432.000000 volume=1296.000000 parts=1`, and two lines `mass E1 area=432.000000 faces=2` at z = 6 and 9; its Provenance output starts with `SingleZonePerFloorType Multipliers=1,3,1`.
   - [ ] The preview shows three volumes, 0 to 3 m, 3 to 12 m (one closed volume without seams at 6 and 9 m), and 12 to 15 m.
   - [ ] *Validate* gives Passed = True. *Convert2BEM*: *Internal Mass* is 0, 1728, and 0 for branches {0}, {1}, {2}.

#### Transform Plan

4. Build a transform: *Rotate* (Transform › Euclidean) with any geometry, plane *XY Plane* at the origin, and angle 30° (switch the *Angle* input to degrees, or give 0.5235987756 rad), then *Move* with the vector 5.3, −2.1, 0, and combine their transforms (*X* outputs, rotation first) with *Compound* (Transform › Util). Connect the canonical plan and the compound transform to *Transform Plan*.
   - [ ] No runtime message. The preview shows the plan rotated 30° counter-clockwise about the origin and moved (bounding box about −3.7, −2.1, 0 to 26.084610, 25.488457, 3).
   - [ ] *Inspect* on the output: its Provenance starts with `TransformPlan RotationDegrees=` followed by 30 (to about 12 digits), `TranslationX=5.3` and `TranslationY=-2.1` (to about 12 digits), with `LinearPlanGenerator …` indented below; its Report lists the same zone and surface IDs as the input plan.
   - [ ] The rotated plan through each of the four simplifiers and each of the four aggregators (Multipliers 1, 3, 1) gives *Validate* Passed = True and no runtime message on the aggregators (16 combinations).
5. Replace the transform by the *X* output of *Scale* (Transform › Affine) with factor 2 about the origin.
   - [ ] *Transform Plan* shows the warning `Not applied (D-070): scaling, shear, or mirroring in plan, a rotation about another axis or scaling along z. Applied: a rotation of 0° about the world z axis and a translation of (0, 0) m in x and y.` and outputs the plan unscaled (bounding box 0,0,0 to 24,18,3).
6. Replace the transform by the *X* output of *Move* with the vector 0, 0, 3.
   - [ ] The warning starts `Not applied (D-070): a translation along z.`; the plan stays at elevation 0.

#### Unsupported storey

7. Give a second *Transform Plan* only the *Move* transform with the vector 5.3, −2.1, 0, pass the moved plan through *No Simplification*, and *Merge* (Sets › Tree) the canonical floor and the moved floor into the Floors of each aggregator with Multipliers 2, 2.
   - [ ] Every one of the four aggregators shows the warning `Warning UnsupportedStorey [L2]: The floor of storey 2 is not fully covered by storey 1: 134.670000 m² of it faces outdoors (D-074).` and still outputs a building; *Stacked Floor Zone Multiplier* also shows `FloorTypeSplit` remarks.
   - [ ] *Validate* on each gives Passed = True with `ok   Building.ExposedFloorArea: reference 134.670000, actual 134.670000`.

#### Heat-transfer options

8. Connect the *Stack Floors* building of the canonical floor (Multipliers 1, 3, 1) to *Convert2BEM*; *Convert2BEM* has the inputs Building, Override, EnableInternalWallHeatTransfer (IWHT), and EnableFloorHeatTransfer (FHT), both options `False` by default.
   - [ ] *Boundaries* contains no `Interzone:` text: the 90 walls between zones and the 48 floors and ceilings between storeys read `Adiabatic`. *Provenance* contains the line `OPTIONS: EnableInternalWallHeatTransfer=False EnableFloorHeatTransfer=False`.
   - [ ] IWHT = True: the 90 walls read `Interzone:<zone ID>` (for example `Interzone:L0/CO` on `L0/ST`), the floors and ceilings between storeys stay `Adiabatic`, and the *OPTIONS* line reads `EnableInternalWallHeatTransfer=True EnableFloorHeatTransfer=False`.
   - [ ] FHT = True (IWHT False): the 48 floors and ceilings between storeys read `Interzone:<zone ID>` and the walls `Adiabatic`.
   - [ ] *Validate* on the building gives the same report with every combination; *Zones*, *Surfaces*, and *Windows* are the same Breps.
9. Connect the *Stacked Floor Zone Multiplier* building of step 1 and set FHT = True.
   - [ ] Its floors and ceilings between storeys stay `Adiabatic` (D-075); with IWHT = True its walls between zones read `Interzone:<zone ID>`.
10. Connect the *Single Zone per Floor Type* building of step 3 and set FHT = True.
   - [ ] The 24 floor and ceiling pieces at 3 m and 12 m read `Interzone:E1`, `Interzone:E0`, or `Interzone:E2`; with FHT = False they read `Adiabatic`.
```

</Project>
