## Exit criteria

- ADR-013 exists, is accepted by the controller under D-075 (pending the owner's reading), and ADR-009's status line points to it.
- D-068: every aggregator builds on `Storeys.Assemble`; the zone multiplier takes its pieces from the stack with pieces between storeys adiabatic (`MultiplierTakesTheDesignationsOfItsStoreysFromTheStack`); `ResolveRepresentative` is gone. D-074: an overhanging or moved storey gives one `UnsupportedStorey` warning with its uncovered area and the building still validates (`AnOverhangingStoreyIsReportedAsUnsupported`, `AStoreyMovedOffTheStoreyBelowIsUnsupported`); setbacks and rounding slivers do not (`SetbacksAndIdenticalStoreysAreSupported`, `RoundingSliversOfARotatedSetbackDoNotMakeAStoreyUnsupported`).
- D-071: `StackedFloorZoneMultiplier`, `SingleZonePerFloorType` (zones `E{i}`, slabs within an entry as internal mass, pieces between entries interzone), and `SingleZoneBuilding` (zone `BUILDING`) exist; `FloorAreaMultiplier` and `SingleZoneMerged` are gone; *Stacked Floor Zone Multiplier* and *Single Zone per Floor Type* keep their GUIDs, *Single Zone Building* has `50965154-e31e-4730-b61c-15a6f40bee6f`.
- D-070, D-076: `PlanTransform` keeps area, volume, wall area, and glazing, turns façades with the geometry, and records `TransformPlan` in provenance; *Transform Plan* (`57c17b9c-04b4-4595-9150-bb923877d95e`) applies only the rotation about z and the x-y translation and warns about the rest; all 64 rotated simplifier × aggregator cases validate without warnings.
- D-069: `HeatTransferOptions.Exported` gives the four combinations; *Convert2BEM* has IWHT and FHT, default `False`, and records them in *Provenance*; the building and its validation do not depend on them.
- Previews: unmodelled storeys of the zone multiplier in transparent grey, multi-part zones as merged volumes, computed from core data; *Preview Location* moves them all.
- 28 icons drawn by `scripts/make_icons.py`; the two renamed ones byte-identical to their S4.1 files.
- Snapshots: five byte-identical, two renamed byte-identical, one new; `scripts/verify.ps1` prints `VERIFY PASSED`; tests: 136 core, 19 generators, 163 integration on `net8.0`; 0 warnings.
- The S4.2 smoke test is recorded in the close-out commit, with the items left for a person named.
- Version 0.4.2, local tag `v0.4.2`; AGENTS.md, README, the roadmap Progress line, pipeline block, and layout, and the decision-log entry D-078 are written; this plan is ticked and marked Done; nothing is pushed (D-075).

## Notes for the reviewer

1. **Source.** Every code block was taken from a verified reference implementation built on `main` at `79f5094` (D-068 to D-075; `main` is now `b8bba9f`, which adds only the D-076 entry), 21 commits in the order of this plan. Three late commits of the reference are folded into the task that owns their files, so that execution produces the end state directly: the rotated-plan rules of ADR-013 (recorded as D-076 on `main` before this plan) into Task 1, the final wording of the *Transform Plan* warning into Task 13, and the vertical-run preview into Task 15. Every task was then replayed on a clone of `b8bba9f` from this plan's file contents: the red errors of Tasks 2, 5, 6, 7, 9, and 11 (including line and column numbers), the failing tests and messages of Tasks 2 (Step 5), 5 (snapshot), 8, 9, and 10, the received snapshot of Task 5 (byte-identical to Step 6), the counts after every task, a 0-warning build after every task, and the 28 icons (byte-identical to the reference) are observed results; the replayed end state equals the reference apart from line endings.
2. **Slices and branches.** The roadmap has no slice list for S4.2; this plan uses seven slices and a close-out, named after D-004: `docs/decisions-adr-013`, `feature/aggregators-assembled-stack`, `feature/plans-transform`, `feature/convert-heat-transfer-options`, `feature/grasshopper-aggregation-transform`, `feature/grasshopper-s4.2-icons`, `docs/architecture-aggregation-transform`, and `chore/repo-s4.2-close-out`. Merges rebase onto local `main`; nothing is pushed in this run, and the tag stays local (D-075).
3. **ADR-013 is accepted by the controller (D-075), not by the owner.** D-073 requires acceptance before any S4.2 code; D-075 lets the controller accept it, provisionally. Task 1, Step 5 records the check against D-068 to D-076; the owner's reading stays open, as it did for ADR-009 (D-060).
4. **Designation on the assembled stack changes no number.** For every building S4.1 accepts, `Storeys.Assemble` gives the same pieces as the per-method resolution did, because the representative storey below always had the floor of the storey physically below. `MultiplierTakesTheDesignationsOfItsStoreysFromTheStack` and the setback cases therefore pass in Task 2, Step 5 already (they characterise the rule rather than drive it); the overhang cases and the overhang row of `VaryingFootprints…` are what fail first.
5. **Zone multiplier: pieces between storeys stay adiabatic whatever *EnableFloorHeatTransfer* says** (D-075 default, ADR-013 option a). They are `Adiabatic` in the building, not only on export, so FHT changes nothing for *Stacked Floor Zone Multiplier* (smoke test item 7). Ground, roof, and exposed pieces come from the stack and lie on × 1 storeys.
6. **Renames.** ADR-002 and ADR-007 keep the name `SingleZoneMerged` and ADR-009 the names `FloorAreaMultiplier` and `SingleZoneMerged`: they are accepted history; ADR-009 gets only the status line. The S2–S4.1 smoke-test checklists keep the old component names (D-064); the S4.2 checklist's preamble says how saved definitions open. Test names follow the renames (`ZoneMultiplierCarriesMultipliersAndOrderedBoundaries`, `SingleZoneBuilding…`). The snapshot contents are byte-identical because `TextReport.Describe` prints no operation name.
7. **The *Single Zone Merged* GUID now carries other semantics.** D-071 keeps `506d5037-…` for *Single Zone per Floor Type*; a definition saved before S4.2 therefore opens with one zone per floor entry instead of one zone for the building (D-064 allows this). Between Task 4 and Task 12 that component runs `SingleZoneBuilding`, which is exactly its old behaviour.
8. **Intermediate files.** Task 2 changes `FloorAreaMultiplier.cs` and `SingleZoneMerged.cs`, which Tasks 3 and 4 rename and replace, so that every commit builds and every red phase is observable; Tasks 3 and 4 change one line each of `AggregatorComponents.cs` for the same reason.
9. **Rotated-plan rules (D-076, ADR-013).** The façade-line test now measures the separation of two walls over their overlap; it is exact for rotated walls and identical for axis-aligned ones, and it does not relax `FacadeCoverage` (walls that do not overlap are no longer on the same line). `PolygonOps.UnionTiles` shares `VertexGrid` with `LayoutSurfaceBuilder`; the builder's output and every snapshot are unchanged. The unsupported-storey test compares the covered area with the floor area through `AreaEquals`, while the D-046 split still compares the exposed area with zero, so a rounding sliver of a rotated setback (about 1.4e-5 m² in `RoundingSliversOfARotatedSetbackDoNotMakeAStoreyUnsupported`) gives no warning but is still not multiplied. The sliver stays in the building as a tiny `Outdoors` floor piece: it is not repaired (GLOBAL.md quality rule 6), and validation compares it with the same sliver in the stacked reference.
10. **Transform Plan.** The decomposition reads a Rhino `Transform` and therefore lives in `Lod.Grasshopper` (`PlanMotionDecomposition`, internal); the geometry is moved by the core `PlanTransform`. The rotation is `atan2(M10, M00)`, so a 30° rotation is recorded as `RotationDegrees=29.999999999999996`. The matrix entries, which are dimensionless, are compared within `ToleranceSettings.Angle` (1e-6), and the z translation within `Distance`; no tolerance is added. The warning is a runtime message without a diagnostic code; the plan is still produced with the applied parts (GLOBAL.md quality rule 6: nothing is changed silently). `PlanMotion` uses exact cosines and sines for whole quarter turns, so axis-aligned plans stay axis-aligned and their results stay exact.
11. **Previews.** The unmodelled storeys come from the core (`StackedFloorZoneMultiplier.UnmodelledStoreys`), are passed into `BuildingGoo` by the component, and are only drawn by the Goo. A multi-part zone is drawn as its vertical runs (`VerticalRuns`, preview-only, in `Lod.Grasshopper/Convert`): parts of one outline stacked on each other are extruded once, so the usual building needs no boolean union. Only runs of different outlines are united with `Brep.CreateBooleanUnion` at `ToleranceSettings.Distance` × unit scale; where that fails, as for the setback in smoke test item 8, the runs are drawn as they are (two solids). The building data and the *Convert2BEM* outputs keep one part per storey outline. A first version united the parts directly; it was replaced in the reference because the union of touching extrusions did not give one closed volume.
12. **Heat-transfer options** live in `Lod.Core.Conversion` so that *Convert2IDF* (S6) applies the same rule. *Convert2BEM* places IWHT and FHT after *Override* and writes the `OPTIONS:` line after the validation report and before the override line, also on blocked solves. The *Boundaries* tallies of smoke test item 7 are the check that only the written boundary changes; *Zones*, *Surfaces*, and *Windows* keep their counts.
13. **Tests changed, not only added.** `VaryingFootprintsKeepExposedAreasWithoutMultiplyingThem` gains the argument `unsupported`; the S4 overhang row now expects the warning; aggregator test names follow the renames; the simplifier and aggregator switches move from `AggregatorTests` to `Pipeline` (Task 7). No test is removed or weakened, and no tolerance or validation check changes.
14. **D-075 defaults as implemented:** pieces facing a represented but unmodelled storey adiabatic whatever FHT says (note 5); unmodelled storeys transparent grey (`150, 150, 150`, transparency 0.85); zones `E{i}` named "Floor type i", and `BUILDING` kept; nicknames Stack, ZMult, 1ZType, 1ZBldg, and TPlan for *Transform Plan* (chosen in the reference); provenance `TransformPlan RotationDegrees=… TranslationX=… TranslationY=…`; ADR-013. The only new diagnostic code is `UnsupportedStorey`.
15. **Documents.** The research brief and `domain-model.md` name no aggregator class and are unchanged; `validation.md` gets the façade-line rule and the new tests. README "Status", AGENTS.md "Project state", the roadmap Progress line, pipeline block, and layout, the decision-log entry, the version, the plan ticks (D-051), and the tag are all in Task 20. The roadmap's S4.2 stage row, S5's dependency on S4.2, the diagram, the sentence below it, the detailed-plans row, and the revision note v6 are committed with this plan.
16. **Smoke-test values.** The expected values of Task 19 were observed in three scripted headless Rhino 8.25 sessions (D-056) on the reference: registration, inputs, *Transform Plan*, and the unsupported storey on the build before the vertical-run preview, the 16 rotated combinations and the heat-transfer tallies on the same build, and the previews on the final build. The *Stacked Floor Zone Multiplier* message list of item 6 was cut off in the log after the two `FloorTypeSplit` remarks; its Warning state shows the `UnsupportedStorey` warning, which `AnOverhangingStoreyIsReportedAsUnsupported` and `AStoreyMovedOffTheStoreyBelowIsUnsupported` check in code. The viewport look, the toolbar icons, the transform built from Grasshopper components, the *Inspect* lines, and the per-surface *Boundaries* texts were not observed there.
17. **`check_plans.py` false positives.** As in S4.1, the `make_icons.py` docstring line that names the Pillow version that drew the icons is flagged as a possible attribution; it names the image library, not an author. The Global Constraints line that states the no-attribution rule is flagged too.
